“Macaque wait, don't go in there!” Tang tried.

“I’m going to kill Wukong if he called the damn paparazzi into our home!” Macaque swore and turned to give a firm glare at Bai He and MK. “You two stay out here!”

“Baba!”
“Macaque!”

But the ebony monkey only slammed the door behind him as he stormed inside. He quickly made his way towards where he could hear voices and let out a loud bellow of his own in warning.

“Wukong!”

He then ran into the foyer, glare ready and fists clenched only to stop short when he saw Azure Lion standing with the golden monkey.

“Azure?” Macaque tilted his head then glanced around looking to see if he could spot anyone else.

“Macaque?!” Azure boggled, stepping away from Wukong and glancing between the two with an unreadable expression.

“I can explain!” Wukong rushed to say, his hands held up in surrender as he looked at the lion.

“What are you doing here?” Macaque questioned, not noticing how Azure’s eyes doubled in size at the question. The ebony monkey was too busy listening and looking for anyone else that may be in the house, but seeing and hearing nothing else he tilted his head at the lion. “Wait, are you Al?”

“Macaque can you give us 5 minutes?” Wukong’s tone was tight as he looked between Azure and his ex.

Azure meanwhile was gapping at the ebony monkey. Still in disbelief at being asked what he was doing at his boyfriend's home.

“Is this the emergency?” Azure asked Wukong as he gestured to Macaque.

“Emergency?!” Macaque’s tone was nothing but offended as he folded his arms and glared at Wukong.

“Partially!” Wukong answered and tried to ignore the sound of disbelief/anger from the monkey behind him. “Mac can you please just give us 5 minutes alone?!”

“This is going to take longer than 5 minutes Wukong!” Azure growled. “Why is your ex-husband in your house?!”

Macaque now glared at Azure. He had known the actor, meeting and talking with him during the various shows, meet-and-greets, and events he accompanied Wukong to. They never formed a deep friendship but they knew of each other. He figured all the actors and “stardom-friends” (as Macaque had dubbed them) would take Wukong’s side in the divorce. But questioning why he was here, in his old home, right in front of him was more than a bit rude and insulting. Who was Azure to question why he was here?!

“Why would he need to explain that to you?!”

“Macaque!” Wukong turned to try and reason with the darker monkey to give him and Azure some alone time so he could continue his talk with the man. However turning his back to Azure he missed the vicious glare the lion gave Macaque as he boomed.

“Because I’m his boyfriend!”

Macaque looked stunned, he let out a very small gasp and he almost seemed to rock back by the announcement. However it was his eyes that (for some reason) hurt Wukong the most. Wukong could see, actually see, Macaque closing himself off. Whatever small truce or understanding the two were working towards, he could see closing up and vanish…and it hurt. The ebony monkey’s eyes moved from Azure to Wukong before holding the golden monkey’s gaze. Wukong felt an invisible weight fall right on top of him. Like a mountain had been dropped on his back and he could only gulp audibly under the pressure of it.

Then Macaque’s shoulders rose, and Wukong could only watch as more of Macaque’s walls went up. With a “tsk” the ebony monkey slapped on a mean smirk.

“Well Wukong’s never been good at explaining things or talking about things till it all blows up and then he runs away.” Macaque spat, still not looking away from Wukong. The golden monkey wanted to say something snarky but the mountain on top of him prevented him from doing so. He felt like he got caught doing something he shouldn't have, when really he didn’t, but his brain kept telling him he did. “So this should be a fun talk for you two.” He finally glanced back to Azure as his tail whipped up behind him.

He then did an about face before stopping and gesturing to the broken vase on the floor. “Clean that up before the kids get hurt.”

“Mac!” Wukong finally got out but was unsure what else to say. His whole body felt like it jolted at Macaque’s plural use of the word ‘kids’. He prayed Al didn’t pick up on that, but with his luck he was sure the lion did.

The black monkey only walked away, a lazy wave of his tail was given as he added on “I’ll be in the bedroom.”

“Bedroom?!” Azure glared at Macaque then Wukong who immediately turned back to the lion to try and explain and placate him. “Bedroom?!” Azure repeated in an angrier and offended tone.

Meanwhile Macaque walked away with a victorious smirk as he heard Azure’s exclamations. However the smirk quickly fell away into an angry and pained expression. He quickly shook his head and made his way to the small gym they had in the basement. He hoped his bedroom comment got Wukong in more shit. He hoped him effortlessly telling Wukong what to do bothered the stupid lion! He hoped reminding Azure him and Wukong had kids together, and were tied together forever pissed him off! He hoped Wukong gets his ass beat by his…

He had to physically stop and take a deep breath and try to collect himself as his emotions rolled within himself. He needed to let all this pent up emotions out and he needed to do it now!

Meanwhile, Wukong had quickly tried to explain away the bedroom comment to Azure.

“He means his bedroom, not like-”

“HIS bedroom?!” Azure boomed in response. “Why does your ex-husband have his own bedroom in your home?! Why is he even here?!”

“It’s complicated.” Wukong tried weakly.

“Then explain it!” Azure demanded however, seeing Wukong wince the lion tried to soften his tone. “Baby please, tell me what’s going on. Why is Macaque” he spat the name “Here? With his own room?”

Wukong let out a huge breath of air and reluctantly began to explain. “Just keep in mind this can’t get out, like I know we had an NDA and all but really.” The monkey locked eyes with Azure and glared at the lion. “This information doesn’t leave my estate. Period!”

Azure felt his fun rising in frustration at this, but he buried it with more patience. He folded his arms but nodded at his boyfriend in order to nudge him to continue.

With that nod Wukong dropped the glare and it was replaced with nervous gestures and faces. “So you know how MK was adopted by Macaque and myself?” The lion nodded, “Well you know how you’ve never seen Mac around MK even after our divorce?”

“Yes, he abandoned you and your son.” Azure voiced, hoping to also reiterate to his boyfriend why the dark monkey was a bad person and thus should not be here now.

“Yeah well,” Wukong rubbed the back on his head. “That was actually because we didn’t just adopt MK…we adopted a baby daughter as well and…well…” Azure’s mouth dropped open in shock, as his eyes boggled so Wukong decided to rush the rest of the explanation hoping that would somehow lessen the shock. “Mac got full custody of her and I got full custody of MK. But they met up at camp and put everything together. And then they sprung this family reunion on us! So Mac, our kids, and me are trying to figure out each other and what we’re going to do going forward!”

Azure still only looked shocked, he leaned back and let the door support his weight as he grabbed his forehead. His brain trying to process all this new information.

“That’s why I needed you to stay away cause this is all so complicated and I can’t afford any…” he gestured to Azure “distractions.”

“Wow.” Azure breathed as he thought everything over. He glanced over to the nervous monkey and steeled himself. “That’s…that’s alot.”

Wukong let out a nervous laugh “Tell me about it.”

“OK, alright.” Azure straightened himself up. “So you have a daughter?”

“Yeah!” Wukong beamed, “She’s fantastic! But this also a lot for her so-”

“This is alot for anyone.” Azure cut in. “How’s MK?”

“He’s fine, he’s been pushing for us to get along and reconnect.” Wukong missed how Azure’s eyes narrowed at that, but the blue lion’s face quickly morphed back into one of understanding and patience. “And Mac’s been…complicated, but it’s just been crazy.”

“I bet.”

“Yeah, so-” Wukong gave a weak gesture towards the door. “That’s why-”

“You’ve been pushing me away?”

Wukong flinched at that, and Azure’s eyes narrowed at the motion.

Wukong’s talk yesterday with Macaque flashed in his mind. Was he just repeating his past mistakes? Pushing Azure away like he did with Mac? And he once again didn’t even notice; instead he kept justifying it. With Mac he justified it was to build his career, with Azure it was to protect his what, reputation?

Azure waited a second before continuing, “Baby, don’t push me away! I need to be here for you and for MK and now for your daughter. I want to be part of this family! Please don’t push me away from it!” Azure pleaded as he grabbed the golden monkey’s hands. Wukong looked unsure still but was clearly wavering. “Or…” Azure took a step back from Wukong and loosened his hold. “Do you not think we’re that serious? Do you think of our relationship as just a fling?”

“No! No!” Wukong gripped Azure’s hands and went closer to the lion. “It’s not that at all!”

“Then let me in! Let me be here for you!” Azure pressured and Wukong bit his lip.

-

“So I’m gonna go see what the drama with this mystery Al is all about!” Mei announced as she went to go inside, but her path was blocked by both Tang and Pigsy.

“No! No! We should just stay out here!” Tang yelled in a panicked tone.

“No you are not!” Pigsy barked at the same time as Tang spoke.

“Come on!” Mei whined. “I need to know what’s going on.”

“No you don’t! What you need is food, and if you ever want to eat in this house again you’ll all keep your keisters out here!” Pigsy finished with his hands on his hips, his glare passing over all the kids. Each kid pouted and deflated but the threat worked as Mei plopped down on a lawn chair.

“But what if Baba is beating up Dad?” Bai He asked worriedly.

“Tang will go check on them.” Pigsy answered. Said man whipped his head over to the pig demon and gawked at him.

“What?!”

“You heard me.” Pigsy made a shoo-ing motion. “Go make sure they aren’t killing each other.” Tang let out a pitiful whine but relented, though he did drag his feet as he made his way back into the house.

As he made his way through the downstairs he saw a room light was on that wasn’t previously. Curious, and wanting to delay the Mac and Wukong confrontation, he went to investigate. Turns out the room was a huge gym with weights, various exercise equipment, and even some gymnastic equipment. The room also contained his boss, who was currently beating the shit out of a punching bag.

Tang froze in the doorway but knew he couldn’t just turn tail and leave. However he didn’t know what to say.

“Don’t say anything!” Macaque barked, making Tang jolt in place. “I know it’s you Tang!” a particularly hard punch made the bag sway. “And I mean it, just-” another hard punch “keep quiet!” He growled.

Tang nodded wordlessly but found he still couldn’t move. Instead he stood in the doorway as Macaque kept unleashing his emotions via various punches, jabs, and kicks.

“I’m sorry.” Tang whispered against his better judgment and Mac was so tempted to throw his water bottle at the man.

“Just shut up! I don’t need any advice” he spit the last word “and that conversation from this morning never happened!” He glared. “We are here to connect with MK and Bai He, figure out what to do now that they know each other, and then we’re going home!”

Tang only nodded as Mac drank the rest of his water.

Chapter End Notes

Two steps forward and six back, oh the drama!

You can see Azure’s subtly manipulation come to play here, and don’t you worry that will come up more and more. After all Azure’s got a great thing going and I don’t think he’s keen to give any of it up.

Also fleshing out chapters while being a new mom is impossible D: So sorry for the long waits but yeah that’s gonna be a thing.

Feel free to come talk to me on Tumblr if you want spoilers or tidbits or whatever for this fic! I wont spoil on Ao3 but I will on tumblr lol >:3

Chapter 7: Promise

Chapter Notes

Sorry this took almost a whole year! But fun fact being a parent leaves you with literally zero time to do anything other than care for baby and survive lol. Still wouldn't trade this for the world but yes updates will be slow.

Again if you have questions or want spoilers hit up my tumblr, same username :D

See the end of the chapter for more notes

“Bai He?” The blue lion demon asked with a tentative knock on said young girl's bedroom door.

Bai He looked up from her journal and gave the lion a look that screamed ‘go away’.

“They wanted me to tell you dinner’s about to be done.” Azure said in a forced light tone as he opened the door more.

After a brief stare down Bai He focused back on her journal, all but dismissing the man.

Azure hesitated before coming further into the girls bedroom and squatted down near the young girl who gave the man a wary look.

“Actually I was hoping we could talk.”

“I don’t want to talk.” Bai He groaned as she closed up her journal and clutched it close to herself. The need to flee, to just go off and be on her own was gripping her and as she was about to get up to go leave, Azure rushed out.

“It’s just, you and I are alot alike! In this situation I mean!” He rushed then pointed between themselves. “I don’t know you enough to know if we share other similarities.”

That piqued the girl's interest so she sat back down “What do you mean?”

“Wukong tells me that you didn’t know about him, so all of this,” He gestured around and towards the door. “Is news to you.” He then gestured to himself. “It’s the same for me, I didn’t know about any of this when I started dating Wukong.”

“You knew about MK.” Bai He retorted, and the man nodded in agreement. “And that Dad was married to Baba.”

“I knew they divorced, yes.” Azure agreed. “But I never knew about you, or that Macaque would choose now to make an appearance. To my knowledge he left and was never to be seen on this coast again.” His tone held some bitterness and agitation in it as he explained but overall he tried to come off as approachable.

Bai He bit her lip as she thought all that over. “...That makes sense.”

“To be honest.” Azure got up and sat next to Bai He. “It’s a bit alienating isn’t it?”

“Alienating?” Bai He tilted her head at the word.

“I mean it feels like we’re the odd ones out. They left us out of the loop. Didn’t feel the need to share information with us. Pushed us away!” Bai He whipped her head at the last sentence and gripped her journal tightly.

“It definitely feels like they pushed us away.” She acknowledged after a few seconds of inner turmoil.

“Well we have each other.” Azure smiled in a welcoming manner. He then leaned closer, “We can feel alone together.” He held out a paw for a hand shake and Bai He smiled as she took it.

“Alone together!”

“Alright!” Azure beamed. He then pushed up from the bed and gestured for the young girl to follow him. “Come on, oh! And if you ever need to talk, you know I can relate so please feel free…” He trailed off and gestured between the two of them. “But no pressure,” He eased back when the raven seemed to be getting defensive. “But since I’m the only one you can really relate to, if you need to talk I’ll be there.”

“...thanks.”

The two went down for another tense dinner with the whole gang. It had been a few days since Azure had all but moved into the mansion. It caused the peace that was in place to break and be replaced with tense and stress with almost every interaction between all the adults. Now Azure wasn’t staying over during the night but he would come over every day and stay all day.

MK kept his cheery disposition, trying his best to be open to his Dad’s new boyfriend while also showing his Baba that he wasn’t being replaced or anything. Meanwhile Bai He had become more reclusive, much like Macaque, barely talking and generally avoiding everyone. Mei had wanted to stick around (honestly to watch the drama unfold, and be there for Bai He and MK) but she was only able to stay the first night before she had to leave for home. And she couldn’t come over everyday. Pigsy’s dislike of Azure was obvious; and it was pretty pointed that Pigsy, Macaque and Tang began spending a lot more time together since Azure’s arrival.

-

MK closed his bedroom door and finally allowed the smile to drop off his face as he went and face planted on his bed. He was exhausted. Another day filled with him trying and failing to keep the peace. He grabbed a pillow and muffled his face into it to let out a long groan. He wanted to scream! But he knew he’d be too loud and he couldn’t risk the others hearing.

Was he the smartest kid on the planet? No! But he wasn’t stupid! He knew things were a mess. Dad suddenly had various and oddly timed jobs he had to go to, Baba suddenly lived in the shadows or something with how often he vanished, and Bai He was shutting everyone out. Well everyone but Azure apparently, since she was talking with him at dinner tonight. But she wasn’t talking to him, Baba, or Dad! Pigsy kept saying snide remarks to which Mac would laugh, Dad would growl, and Azure would look sad. And MK was exhausted! Thankfully Tang helped out at tonight's dinner, mainly by hogging and steering the whole dinner time conversation about some history of something or other (MK zoned in and out throughout it).

He envied Bai He for how she just shut them all out without a care for its effects…maybe that wasn’t fair. But it wasn’t fair that he was left to be the peacemaker, to be alone in keeping things civil! He thought they were a team! They were supposed to ride out the remainder of summer together so they knew they’d be able to see each parent no problem. But this was just a big mess of problems!

He had to keep persevering; no matter how much he just wanted to scream and shout. He had to keep smiling and keep trying. No one else was going to. Which brought up the thought that kept beating on MK’s stubborn head.

‘What was the point?’

Everyone else seemed fine letting this chaos turn into a bigger mess, to fall apart. Separate and wash their hands of each other. Why was it all on MK?! He didn’t even know what to do! He was just a kid too! He was never even able to get his Dad to admit to Mac existing! So how could he fix things with his Dad and Baba?! And if everyone else was fine with it falling to ruin, why should he keep pushing himself for this?!

Springing up, MK flung his door open to head to the gym downstairs.

“MK!” Azure greeted with a smile, “I was just about to see if you wanted to talk.”

“Sorry in a rush! See you later!”

“Wha-?” Azure’s eyes followed the blur that was the young man. “Uh…ok.” He finished confused with a scratch to the back of his head.

-

Macaque had finished getting changed into his work out clothes and was hesitantly approaching the gym. He had heard the sounds of someone else in the workout room and was worried about who he’d run into. Peeking in, he cocked an eyebrow when he saw it was MK, not in workout clothes but the same outfit he wore earlier and the boy was attacking the punching bag erratically. Clearly just throwing random hits and varying degrees of strength behind each punch.

“MK?”

Said man jerks bodily and looks towards Macaque. Huffing, the young man shakes out his hands and tries to smile but it falls flat.

“What are you doing here?” Macaque asks as he comes fully into the room.

“Working out.” MK answers easily with a frail smile.

“In that?” Macaque points at MK’s outfit with his tail.

“Psssh yeah.”

“It’s cotton.”

“So?”

“So you don’t wear cotton when you plan to work out because it absorbs sweat, making you heavier and smellier.” Macaque explains confidently with a smirk.

“Well I do!” MK says defensively.

“No, you're down here because you needed to get something out.” The ebony monkey insisted with his arms crossed.

“No!” MK denied with a pout.

“What’s bothering you?”

“Nothing!”

“MK, what’s wrong?”

“You know what’s wrong!” MK finally yelled. “Everything’s wrong!” Mac remained calm as MK yelled and shoved at the punching bag before pushing away from it and began to pace around. “None of you are even trying! Dad’s running! You're hiding! Bai He gave up! I’m all alone! And I’m tired!” MK stilled and his shoulders slumped in exhaustion. “None of you are even trying anymore.”

Macaque tsk-ed before going over and wrapping his tail around one of MK’s wrists. “You're right.” MK looked at his Baba and Mac’s tail shook MK’s wrist in comfort. “It’s not fair that we stopped trying. I’m sorry we…I’m sorry I put that all on you.” MK looked so hopeful and Mac pulled the young man into a hug. “You don’t have to do this alone.”

“Are you going to stop hiding?”

“Can’t promise I won’t hide away at times, especially when that bright blue blight is around.” The dark monkey growled.

“Baba are you jealous cause he’s spending time here?”

“He shouldn’t even be here!” Mac immediately snapped back. “How long have they been dating and Wukong thinks it's fine for him to be around you and Bai He?!”

“Well Dad said him and Azure have an NDA and that Tripitaka said-”

Macaque let out a loud and long groan cutting MK off. “One problem at a time so don’t bring up that man.”

“Oh right.” MK forgot that even his Dad had said Tripitaka was still a sore point between his parents. “Sorry.”

“Look kiddo.” Mac quickly directed the conversation “You don’t have to worry anymore, you’re not alone. I’m here for you and Bai He and I’m not going anywhere.”

“Promise?”

“I promise.” He then shoved MK away and pushed him towards the door. “Not go get changed and then come back down. I’ll show you how to really punch your emotions out.”

“Alright!” MK smiled and took off to do as he was told.

Chapter End Notes

Fun fact I do not like this chapter lol. It was so hard to flesh out and I still do NOT like it but it hits the plot points if not clunkily and roughly.

From the show we know Mac pays a lot of attention to MK and seems to know his inner thoughts or at least what MK needs to hear. So in here I have Mac picking up on MK’s ques very quickly (probably because they’re similar to Wukong’s old tells).

And Bai He doesn’t know it but she just played exactly into where Azure wants her. Trusting him and thinking he is the only one she can relate and open up to.

Chapter 8: Avoiding the Mess

Chapter Notes

See the end of the chapter for notes

Wukong furiously scrubbed at the back of his neck as he got out of the shower. Typically he bathed in the morning so he could dry on his patio while eating breakfast. However he knows that now, that’s not the best idea. He knew he was being a coward but he just couldn’t face everything. So it was nighttime showers with wet sheets and scraggly fur until…well just until he was ready.

Wrapping himself up in his last clean robe he walked into his room while scrubbing at his fur with his towel. Which is why he missed the dark monkey sitting on his bed.

“Done running?”

“ACK!” Wukong jumped and quickly whipped his head in Macaque’s direction. “Damnit Macaque!” The golden monkey put a hand to his chest. “You scared the shit out of me! I forgot how sneaky you can be.” He gave a playful glare at the dark monkey who hadn’t stopped looking smug; and if anything now looked more smug.

“You're just oblivious.” Macaque replied easily but then held up a hand to look at his nails. “Though yes I am good at sneaking around.”

Wukong rolled his eyes and then continued to scrub his mane dry with his towel. The dark monkey quirked an eyebrow and glanced about the room, noting the numerous towels and robes in the hamper and the brushes filled with fur (clearly in need of a cleaning themselves). Smoothly getting up, Macaque walked over to the brushes and began picking out the fur trapped in them. Wukong observed him silently, waiting to see what his ex’s next move would be.

“I mean,” Macaque said with a haughty air that already had Wukong wanting to roll his eyes. “I knew you would run away.” And just like that, the eye roll and annoyance was replaced by shame and agitation. “But to even change up your routine.” The dark monkey used the now clean brush to gesture to the laundry hamper. “How long do you plan on running?” He asked, suddenly serious.

Wukong scoffed and threw his towel in the hamper and went to sit on his bed. “Cause you have been so present!” The sarcasm dripped off each word and from Macaque’s eyebrow twitch, it hit its mark.

Macaque opened his mouth to fire back a retort then seemed to hold himself back. As if he remembered something. Whatever thought crossed the dark monkey’s mind, it made him deflate. Macaque huffed out a frustrated breath then went over to the bed, and gestured for Wukong to move over.

“The audacity.” The golden monkey grumbled as he scooted over so Macaque could lean against one side of the bed and him the other “That’s not even your side.”

He missed the fleeting smile that managed to sneak its way onto Macaque’s face before the dark monkey banished it with a scowl. Then with a tone of agitation and borderline anger he said.

“If you want to make excuses for running away you can give those to Azure, but I am done entertaining your cowardice.”

“Hey!”

“We have two children here who are dealing with a lot. Not only did they each find out about us, but now your new boytoy too! It’s too much!”

“I know that!”

“Then why are you running away?!”

“I’m not!”

Macaque’s anger dropped to a deadpan look. “Really? You’re going to try and deny it?”

Wukong opened his mouth, face determined to defend himself…but he couldn’t. His expression fell and he ran a hand down his face in defeat.

“...I…I was trying to avoid all this.”

“Avoiding it doesn’t make it go away Wukong!” Macaque argued.

“No!” The golden monkey held up his hands in surrender and quickly tried to explain.”No I mean I was trying to avoid all this mess! You think I didn’t know adding Azure would make this even harder?!” He gave a crazed look to Mac who only blinked at his outburst. “I was trying to avoid this! I told Azure over and over, and OVER! Do not come over! Why do you think I raced to him when Pigsy said he was here?! I knew that would be too much for MK and Bai He! I was trying to do the right thing! I was!” Wukong sounded almost desperate and manic as he continued to explain/vent. “But how can I tell him to stay away when I’m having my ex-husband live with me?! What type of partner does that make me?! I mean that looks awful! And he wanted to meet MK, like really wanted to! But I had Bai He! And she didn’t need to meet my boyfriend! She’s just meeting me! But then he was saying how I’m pushing him away, and I thought of how I pushed you away! And I-”

“WUKONG!”

Macaque’s six ears were all splayed out and really stood out beautifully against the dull color of the bedroom walls. The golden monkey had forgotten how they’d splay out at the side of Mac’s face sometimes; and how the colors seemed more vibrant when they did that. He then registered the hand on his chin, which had forced him to look directly at the dark monkey. Wukong let out a small gasp as he realized how close their faces were. And -purely on reflex- his eyes glanced to the distance between their…chins.

“Finally.” Macaque sighed out and immediately backed his face away from the other monkey. “You were spiraling.” Macaque then let go of Wukong’s face and twirled his fingers, “Turn around.”

“What?” Wukong felt lost and his head (again purely on instinct of course) chased after the hand that released him.

“Turn around.” Macaque once again ordered. “Actually, take off your robe.”

“What?!” Wukong reeled back, clutching his robe closed and mentally was completely lost now.

“I’ll cover my eyes but put on some shorts and then put your back to me.” True to his word Macaque closed his eyes and even covered them with his hand. Leaving the golden monkey on the bed next to him, still just as confused as before.

Wukong glanced around the room as if it would provide answers. “Um…”

The dark monkey let out a long sigh and with one hand still covering his eyes, he raised the other hand that held one of Wukong’s brushes. And for a moment Wukong was…touched? He knew his fur was getting scraggly and he wasn’t taking care of it like he should. Macaque was always the one with the better fur care routine. He would always tell Wukong what he needed to do to treat his fur and would never let the golden monkey get it too messy or out of control.

“Your fur is shit and your mind is too apparently.” Macaque sassed.

And there went being touched and in its place came annoyance.

“Well excuse me for being busy.”

“Busy running.” Macaque teased.

“Busy working, Mister hide-away!” Wukong fired back as he got up and did as instructed. Replacing his robe with some boxers and shorts. Macaque let out a hmm, but otherwise didn’t comment till Wukong’s tail tapped him. “Ready.”

Macaque opened his eyes and immediately began grooming and combing Wukong’s back. And for a time they sat in silence, surrounded only by the sounds of the brush and fingers combing through hair.

It helped. All the racing thoughts that were previously rushing to the surface, desperate to get out of the golden monkey’s brain; they all calmed down. He found himself unwinding. His mind and thoughts were smoothing out. His worries were not gone, but subsided for the moment.

However, Wukong was never a fan of being silent for too long.

“We haven’t done a compliment to each other in a while.” Since Azure arrived actually, but Wukong was not going to point that out. And in fact before Macaque could say something to that effect the golden monkey rushed out. “You’ve always been the best, um groomer? At grooming? I’m not sure how to say it. But yeah, you’re better at it than me.”

Macaque let out a chuckle at Wukong’s antics. “Thank you.”

“...Sooo?” The golden monkey turned to look over his shoulder, to which Macaque just cocked an eyebrow. “Gonna compliment me now?”

The dark monkey rolled his eyes. “Maybe give me a minute.”

“Just checking! Just checking!”

. . .

“....Really?”

“I’m thinking!” The dark monkey fired back, defensive.

“It takes you that long?!”

“You haven’t exactly been my favorite person recently!”

“So?! I can still think of complements for you!”

“Psh! Yeah right!”

“Yuh-huh!”

“Prove it!”

“OK you -OH! Nope! Nuh-uh! Nice try!” Wukong turned around to face the smirking monkey and pointed right at him. “You have to at least give me one complement! Before I inflate your already massive ego!”

“If my ego is massive then what’s yours?”

“Teeny tiny since someone isn’t saying anything nice about me.”

Macaque rolled his whole body in disbelief. “Oh yeah, sure.”

“Come on!”

“Fine!” The dark monkey groaned. “You…” Wukong leaned forward with an eager grin that made Macaque’s eye twitch. “You have,” Wukong’s grin grew wider and so with a smirk Mac finished. “Eyes.”

Wukong’s face fell and Macaque started laughing. The golden monkey gave the dark monkey’s shoulder a shove. “Be serious! Come on!”

“I did you a favor,” He held up the hair brush. “I feel like that covers my compliment.”

“It does not! Argh, you’re impossible!”

Macaque chuckled as he got up and put the brush away. Turning around he watched as Wukong also got up in order to put on a sleeping shirt.

“We should talk with the kids.” The dark monkey offered. “Maybe just have a family day or dinner?”

“Yeah you’re right.” Wukong sighed.

“So no running tomorrow?” Macaque pushed.

Wukong looked over to him, both were serious; the joyful and teasing mood from earlier was not gone but was on the backburner for both of them. He moved closer to the dark furred monkey and gave a nod.

“So long as you don’t hide away anymore.”

Macaque grimaced but also gave a less sure nod. Macaque then gave a teasing grin to Wukong. “Pigsy’s going to kill you with how wet you’ve gotten the floors in here.” he inclined his head towards the bed. “Not to mention the bed.”

“Don’t tell him!” Wukong demanded. “I’ve managed to keep it hidden for now and I can do my own laundry. So he’ll never have to know.”

Macaque rolled his eyes at Wukong. “Alright I’ll keep your secret. But seriously just do at least a simple brush down of your fur before passing out.” The dark monkey pulled one hair from Wukong, who -to his credit- didn’t even flinch. “Your coat will get scruffy if you don’t.”

“Yeah yeah.” Wukong waved off.

Macaque then took that as his cue to leave. Wukong glanced after the dark monkey and sighed after hearing the man make his way down the hall.

That had actually gone well. Better than well! He was tempted to call Sandy to tell him how good him and Macaque handled…well whatever that was.

Wukong glanced at the brush Macaque had used on him and his lips twitched up. It reminded him of the past. Though back then Macaque was more blunt, in that he didn't wait till Wukong was ready. Macaque decided when he was ready to start grooming Wukong and would manhandle the golden monkey if Wukong didn’t get in position fast enough. He’d also do a quick groom with his hands if Wukong was really anxious, like just before an audition.

Wukong smiled softly before letting out a breath to clear his mind and think of what to do tomorrow. Azure would come over again. He doubts he’ll be able to get him to leave for the day but a family dinner would work. He’s sure Azure would listen to him and at least let him have a private dinner.

-

“MK! MK!” Azure chanted as he waved the brunette down.

The young man was just about to make his way over towards the stables where Bai He was waiting. Upon hearing the lion demon the teen paused and turned back to the smiling lion.

“Hey Azure.”

“Good morning MK!” Azure gave a friendly grin and patted MK on the shoulder as he got closer. “I’m on my way out but I didn’t want to leave without saying goodbye.”

“Oh, tha-”

“I know how important that is for children with your background.” Azure nodded solemnly, MK quirked an eyebrow at that but the lion just continued on. “And I wanted to thank you. You’ve been so welcoming and I know things have been…tense to put it mildly.”

“Yeah…” MK agreed awkwardly.

“But through it all you have been so kind and open to me and my relationship with your Dad.” Azure beamed and wrapped an arm around MK’s shoulder. “I can’t tell you how much it means to me!” He gave the brunette a squeeze. “I was so worried about meeting you and wanting to make a good impression. I read so many books about children who had been abandoned and how to approach them-”

“Woah woah!” MK cut in startled as he pushed away from Azure. “Abandoned?!” He gave the lion a bewildered look. “I mean I know I was adopted but-”

“NO!” Azure rushed holding his paws up, a flash of utter panic crossing his face. “No, not about you being adopted! I meant about Macaque and him abandoning you! I never ever meant to insinuate-”

“Baba didn’t abandon me!” MK immediately defended, stepping further away from Azure.

The Blue Lion opened his mouth to speak then closed it and really looked to be thinking over his words. Then with an air of caution he explained.

“Firstly, let me apologize again. I did not in any way mean to insult or insinuate anything about your adoption. That was careless of me, and I am sorry for that.” However instead of giving MK time to respond the buff lion continued talking. “Secondly, I know you and everyone are working on re-connecting, and obviously that’s a bit awkward for me, but I understand and respect it. I’m extremely happy about meeting Bai He and I’ve built a good bond with her, as I’m sure you’ve noticed.” He indicated towards MK who gave a stilted nod. “But the fact of the matter is, Macaque abandoned you, Bai He, and Wukong. He left for New York and however little time that was for, it’s still abandonment.”

MK’s brain stalled at what Azure said, however he was still defensive and his body took an automatic step back when Azure tried to approach him. At his retreat Azure stopped and held up his hands and took a step back.

“I don’t mean to offend. The last thing I want is to hurt you or Bai He!” Azure insisted. “But I want to keep you both safe and unfortunately I am worried Macaque may be setting you up for disappointment.”

“Stop it!” MK yelled. “Just stop it”

The brunette glared hard at Azure. His mind was spinning. And he didn’t want to hear anymore. Who was Azure to insult his Baba?! Macaque had just promised MK he would try harder and literally today, the next day, got a family day with just the 4 of them set up.

“You don’t know my Baba!” MK pointed harshly at Azure. “So you don’t get to say shit about him or us or what happened!” MK’s chest heaved as he stood his ground, however the blue lion's patient and understanding face was causing his anger and righteous fury to slip away. Like water in his palm he tried to hold onto it but it kept escaping, which just frustrated him more. “I have to go, I’m leaving!” He huffed and quickly did an about face.

“I understand, I’ll see you later and I am sorry for upsetting you.” MK glanced back at Azure, his eyes darting around before he nodded and turned back to stalk towards the stables.

He couldn’t keep his thoughts straight. He wanted to be pissed at Azure for what he said about Macaque. However he didn’t lie, everything he said was technically true. But he didn't want to think of his Baba like that! And yes his Dad did tell him how Mac left them for New York and only came back to take Bai He away but that didn't mean he was abandoned…right? Dad said he called and would talk with him while he was in New York. He was working! It was the same as when his Dad would work on location or have to travel or go on tour… Except Mac only called and stayed in contact till he left for good. Then he never tried again. Neither did his Dad try with Bai He, baby or no that was abandonment too! But if Bai He was abandoned by Wukong then Macaque did abandon MK.

The brunette stopped in place as the realization took hold of him.

He had been abandoned.

His birth parents didn’t want him for whatever reason. Then one of his adopted parents, raised him for years and then just left him. And his Dad left Bai He.

Was this why Bai He had pulled away? Did she figure this out before MK? She was smart, smarter than him more than likely. He knew he wasn’t the smartest one around but…is that why? Was he just not as quick to figure out what she already did? Would she leave to? Was that why she was pulling away? Was he just not enough?

Chapter End Notes

Fun fact the teasing about the compliment of the day is like my fav part of this whole chapter!

With Azure here’s more of his manipulations at play but he didn’t think everything through, meaning the whole adoption thing. So he really did panic lol. I just picture him mentally stepping on a very obvious landmine but not noticing it cause he’s too laser focused on his goal -aka get the kids to connect to him more than Mac.

And we can’t have an MK without an arc of MK doubting if he’s worthy/good enough.

Chapter 9: Beach Trip

Chapter Notes

See the end of the chapter for notes

Bai He quickly looked back at MK, who was riding rather dejectedly. Which was odd since he was so excited and hyped up when he heard about their “family fun” day. Bai He was skeptical, well more accurately, she was nervous and apprehensive. And now MK seemed to share that sentiment…maybe, He seemed more upset than anything else.

She had asked him about it but he was rather short with her so she didn’t push. She still wasn’t very good at interacting with people. And was unsure if pushing MK would help, it never felt good to her when everyone kept asking what was wrong and insisting they could talk to her.

Giving MK another look she hoped he would get in a better mood soon. She didn’t like her brother looking upset.

“MK?” The boy looked over to her and she grinned at him, hoping to inspire a smile she rushed out. “Race you around the plum grove!” And then she took off, well took off into a trott. She was still trying to learn the commands.

“Squeeze and lean!” MK called.

She grinned at him and did as directed, and then took off in a gallop. MK laughed and followed behind her. His bad mood seemingly left behind as they raced.

After the race they boarded up the horses, happy to have given them some exercise today. She would really miss them when she went back home to New York. She hoped MK would mail her pictures of them so she could keep tabs on them.

After a quick shower and outfit change the kids made it to the living room where Macaque and Wukong stood. They both indicated they had a surprise planned and guided the kids to the car. Loading them in.

“So Family day is in a car?” MK asked skeptically.

“Yup!” Macaque answered at the same time as Wukong said “Nope!”

“Stop that!” Wukong glared with no real malice. Macaque just chuckled as did the kids.

“We’re going on a mystery trip!” Wukong announced with a flourish as he did jazz hands while holding the steering wheel with his thumbs.

“A mystery trip?” Bai He tilted her head.

“To where?” MK questioned.

“Can’t tell you. Hence the mystery part of the trip.” Wukong teased as he glanced back at both of them through the rear view mirror.

Macaque readjusted himself and turned to look back at the kids. “Don’t get too excited.”

“Hey!” Wukong protested but the dark monkey ignored him.

“We didn’t have a ton of time to prepare. But we think you both will have fun.” He smiled gently and didn’t miss the way MK's smile was fleeting and looked a bit forced.

The car ride proved to be interesting. Macaque had forgotten how bad of a driver Wukong was. While Wukong complained about Macaque’s backseat driving.

“Wukong brake!” Macaque stressed, his fur puffing up as he mimed pressing the break in his passenger seat.

On instinct Wukong began pressing on the brake but then caught himself and scoffed, “I was gonna!”

“When?!”

“Oh we had time!” Wukong said exasperated, he then blew a raspberry at the darker scowling monkey. “You backseat drive too much.”

“I do not!” Mac snapped back and was about to gesture to the car in front of them and go into his defense on Wukong’s driving when he heard Bai He scoff.

“Yeah you do Baba.” The young girl said lightly. Both monkeys whipped their heads toward her. Wukong with a huge grin and Mac with a look of betrayal.

“Bai He! When have I ever-”

“You tell cab drivers how to drive, even in other countries.” She deadpanned, making MK and Wukong burst into laughter.

Macaque folded his arms and sunk into his seat. “I just give them directions.”

“Bullshit.” Wukong whispered to which Mac immediately glared at him for. Wukong sensed the glare and only smiled wider.

“Well excuse me for worrying about safety over worrying about getting anywhere as fast as possible.” Wukong gave the dark monkey a stink eye to which Mac gave a face that said tell-me-I’m-wrong.

“We get there safely.” Wukong responded.

“And only just barely break the sound barrier.” MK muttered, but it was loud enough for all the occupants to hear. And now MK found himself with all his family's eyes on him.

“MK!” Wukong was aghast, his face reflecting one of comical betrayal.

Macaque looked vindicated and Bai He was giving her older brother a smile so MK figured it was his turn to antagonize their parents a bit. So with a sheepish grin he scratched the back of his head and continued.

“I mean don’t get me wrong Dad, we do get where we’re going fast and in one piece. Buuuut half the time my seatbelt is indented into my shoulder and I know Pigsy hates riding with you because of your “granite foot”.” Bai He and Macaque burst out laughing at this.

“Does Pigsy still refuse to let Wukong drive?” Macaque asked, giving the golden monkey a teasing grin.

Said monkey rolled his eyes and answered instead of letting MK answer. “Yeah yeah he still insists on driving. But he’s a control freak…like you.” Wukong tossed the casual insult with a teasing lilt.

Not taking offense, the dark monkey rolled his eyes. “Oh I’m the control freak?”

“Yes.” Wukong, Bai He and MK answered at the same time; making Macaque huff and sink into his seat in exasperation. However when Wukong glanced over to him he saw the small smile the dark monkey had on his face.

Finally the car pulled down a covered and seemingly private road.

“I know where we are!” MK beamed as they drove down the desolate, woodsy, dirt road. Bai He gave him an inquisitive look but MK only beamed at her. “You’ll love it!”

“What is it?”

“It’s a surprise~!” Wukong teased.

However Bai He only pouted and folded her arms. It was obvious to her that once again she was the only one not in the know. Everyone seemed to know where they were going but not her.

“If MK knows then it’s not a surprise anymore.” Macaque deadpanned. He then turned in his seat and smiled at Ba He, who looked hopeful up to her Baba. “We’re going to-”

“I wanna tell her!” MK jumped in excitedly. Wukong snickered as Macaque rolled his eyes.

Bai He turned to her brother and he grabbed her face and directed it towards the windshield. “We’re going to,” he paused for dramatic effect “The BEACH!”

“The beach?”

“Yup!”

“And not just any beach.” Wukong added in. “But our private beach front.”

“Private?” Bai He questioned excitedly and Wukong only nodded.

“Yeah Dad bought it and this stretch of land.” MK waved at all the trees around them. “So we don’t have to worry about paparazzi.”

Macaque whipped his head over to Wukong so fast, the kids worried his neck may have snapped. “You did?”

“Mhmm.” Wukong hummed as he slowed the car. They had approached the part where the tree line thinned and the sandy beach front could be seen. “Rocks and such are around so it’s our private little alcove, but just to be safe.” He looked meaningfully at Macaque at that. “I did buy up all the forest path and surrounding land.”

“You have a private beach?!” Bai He screeched in excitement. “You are so cool Dad!” Wukong soaked up the praise with a smile “Did you guys pack my bathing suit?!”

Macaque seemed too stunned to speak so Wukong answered. “Yes, and MK we packed yours as well. And we packed some other fun stuff hidden in the trunk.”

Bai He and MK high fived and quickly lept out of the parked car. They wasted no time in grabbing all the beach toys, and their clothes and racing towards the waterfront. As the parents left the car they could hear MK explain to Bai He where the changing room and storage shed was.

“I can’t believe you thought of keeping the paparazzi away.” Macaque said and Wukong was tempted to be offended at the statement by the thoughtful and almost stunned look on the dark monkey's face caused him to change his mind.

“Well.” Wukong scratched the back of his head as he went to help grab all the items the kids left behind. “To be honest it wasn’t until MK came out that I realized he shouldn’t…you know, have to worry about going to the beach and getting spied on.”

“Ah!” Macaque intoned as he joined Wukong and grabbed the basket filled with food Pigsy had prepared for them. “That makes sense.”

The beach area had a steady inclining cliff on one side and an array of large boulders further down on the other side. However, that still left a lot of sand and water access. And the sand part of the beach was rather expansive as well; large enough for a volleyball court and still having a bunch of free room. And Wukong did have the space built up, there were two poles for a volleyball net to be hung between, though currently the net was not there. There was a grill station with 2 picnic tables nearby. And near the edge of the treeline and sand area was a sizable storage shed and changing room.

“So what do you think?” Wukong asked, his tone smug.

“Dare I ask why you have no bathroom?” Macaque drawled as he looked over the space.

“Uhhh,” Wukong said, the wind being taken out of his sails. “Well I mean…” His eyes flicked towards the ocean and then to the trees.

Macaque gave Wukong a deadpan stare then shook his head in disbelief. “You're disgusting.”

“Hey!”

MK came out of the storage shed with the volleyball net and Macaque went to go help him hang it up. However the whole time Macaque could feel MK’s eyes on him, but when he looked over towards his son MK would quickly look away or put on that forced smile again. Macaque’s eyebrows knitted in concern at the brunette but couldn’t confront him as he ran off to get changed.

Finally everyone was in their beach attire and Bai He was properly sunscreened, Macaque and Wukong did not need it except for their faces. MK refused, too eager to get in the water he had said; though Macaque felt it was an excuse to run away. It seemed MK and Wukong shared that trait and unfortunately for MK, Macaque knew how to spot it.

The family went to the waterfront and Bai He insisted on building a giant sand castle. Wukong and MK agreed to help, however Macaque pulled MK aside with his tail.

When MK gave him a questioning look the dark monkey indicated with his head to follow him. After walking on the beach for a bit so they were out of Wukong and Bai He’s earshot, Macaque turned to his son.

“Somethings bothering you I can tell.” Before MK could protest or say anything to the contrary, Mac continued. “I’m not sure what it is, but…” MK’s eyes flickered in a panic from the dark monkey to the ground and that’s what finalized his guess “I think it’s about me.” MK flinched and Macaque took a breath (to calm himself or steel himself he wasn’t sure). “So it is about me…”

MK again looked away from Macaque and studied the sand. He wanted to bring up all his revelations from hours before but also…today was supposed to be fun and Macaque was clearly here and present and…

“You’re not going to leave again right?”

“What?!” Macaque gasped, floored at what MK had just asked him.

MK meanwhile hadn’t meant to say it, he didn’t! He wanted to, but he knew he shouldn’t! But it slipped out! So he couldn’t bring himself to look up at his Baba. He just watched as the ocean’s tide grazed the side of his and Mac’s feet. The sand bubbling where some sea creatures had buried themselves.

Macaque meanwhile blinked and quickly composed himself. He then grabbed MK’s shoulder with one hand and ducked to get MK’s eyes back on him.

“MK look at me.” Reluctantly the teenager did. “I promised you yesterday, I’m not going anywhere and that’s true today and everyday after.” MK smiled a small, hopeful thing and Macaque shook the shoulder in his grip. “Have more faith in me bud.” He cajoled.

“Yeah I’m-”

“No.” Macaque pressed a finger to MK’s mouth effectively shutting the young man up. “You and Bai He have been through alot. This doubt and worry I can’t blame you for.” Macaque smiled at MK. “I’ve been watching you through paparazzi shots this whole time but seeing you in person,” He winked. “I’m not giving that up.”

MK smiled wider and threw himself at Macaque, who was caught off guard but recovered quickly enough to keep MK and him from falling over.

“What’s that all about?” Wukong wondered aloud as he watched MK almost topple Macaque over in a hug.

Bai He looked up from where she was packing dry sand on top of some wet sand and frowned. Under her breath she muttered “We’ll never know.”

Wukong raised an eyebrow at his daughter but the young girl just packed the sand down more harshly than she was previously.

“What do you mean by that?” Wukong prodded as he idly fiddled with the sand.

“Nothing.” Bai He said immediately, as if on instinct if the blink and brief reconsideration Wukong saw on her face was any indication.

“You said “we’ll never know.”” Bai He let out a groan and steadfast did not look at Wukong who gently poked her with the tip of his tail. “What do- no” Wukong made a point to stop and re-word his question, catching Bai He’s attention. “...Do you think Mac and MK wouldn’t tell us what just happened with them?” Wukong finally asked slowly, making sure he worded his question correctly.

Bai He quickly looked over towards her brother and Baba who were walking back to the shed and changing room. She then looked back at Wukong who was trying to be patient and not look too eager. He didn’t want to scare her and make her shut down again but he was so eager to get her to open up to him, even if just a tiny bit.

“Maybe MK would.” She smiled slightly. “He talks a lot.” she said in a teasing tone and Wukong cracked a smile at it. “But Baba…” She trailed off and no further explanation was needed, not to Wukong.

“I get it.” The golden monkey agreed. “Your Baba, Macaque, he’s…” Wukong twisted his lips together as he thought of the appropriate word, however one didn’t seem to encompass all he wanted to say. “He likes to keep things close to his own chest.”

“More like locked up tight.” The young girl grumbled.

Wukong meanwhile rocked back onto his heels as he did a quick scan of Bai He. “And that bothers you.” It wasn’t a question and from the young girls knitted together eyebrows he ventured further. “It upsets you.”

“Yeah!” Bai He burst out then quickly double checked to see where MK and the dark monkey were.

“They’re putting sunscreen on MK.” Wukong explained as he and Bai He looked back towards the other two members of their family.

The young girl then looked back at Wukong and although she was hesitant, she felt the same ease as when she first arrived at her Dads home, wash over her. Looking at Wukong who sat there patiently but pointedly eased her into continuing her explanation. “He never talks to me! Not about…emotional stuff.”

“Yeah...” Wukong rubbed the back of his head with a grimace “He’s never been good about talking about that kind of stuff. Neither was I to be honest.” Bai He’s eyebrows knitted together but just as her face was about to fall into disappointment her Dad continued. “But I’m better at it now.” He smiled and scooted closer to his daughter. “Took some time but,” He ruffled Bai He’s hair. “You ever want to talk, you can come to me! I don’t want you to bottle everything up, I know you have been lately, and I don’t want to push you!” He rushed. “But I miss talking with you, I miss you being open with me.”

“I miss it too.” Bai He admits in a whisper, ashamed. However her Dad only smiled wider.

“Whenever you're ready to talk I’m here to listen.”

Bai He stared at Wukong then leaned in and put her weight against him. “Thanks Dad.”

Wukong kissed the top of her head in response. “Welcome sweetheart.”

“MK! We’re not done yet!” The two heard Macaque scream in the distance followed by MK’s laughter and the sound of him running.

MK shot past the father and daughter duo and raced into the surf, boogie board in hand. MK whooped with joy as he swam out to try and catch the next set of waves.

“How far along did you get?” Wukong asked as Macaque joined the two and squatted next to Bai He in the sand.

The dark monkey groaned as he began to straighten the raven girls hair out. “Everything but his face.” He then began to help her build up the sand castle.

“Woah! New record.” Wukong laughs. “He’s the worst at putting on sunscreen, can’t wait to tell Pigsy his record has finally been beaten.”

“What was his record?” The young girl asked as she watched MK fail to catch a wave under his boogie board in time.

“No covering for his face or arms.”

Macaque hummed but Bai He’s eyes took on a look of determination. “I call dibs next time we come here.” She turned to Mac and with a teasing glint declared. “I’m going to beat your record.”

“Oh-ho!” Wukong laughed “A new challenger approaches!”

“Our kids' health should not be a contest.” Macaque deadpans though his tone was still playful, he then throws a challenging smirk to the young girl. “But bring it on”. However his face quickly turns into a frown as the tower he was working on fell apart.

“Gotta build up the base Baba.” Bai He teased.

“The level of sass.”

Squeals then erupted into the air as Mac picked Bai He up and made a show of going to toss her into the water. MK rushing to join in as Wukong leaned back and watched the drama with a smile.

“Save me Dad!” The young girl cried dramatically but MK and Macaque only let out campy villainous laughter in return.

“No one can save you now!” MK boasted dramatically. He then went back to his regular voice. “But seriously, Dad can’t swim so you're really screwed here.”

“Hey!” Wukong’s said, offended.

“Into the water with you!” Macaque yelled as he bodily tossed the young girl into the deeper parts of the water. Her outraged scream only cut off as the water engulfed her.

“Mac!” Wukong snapped “Don’t just toss our daughter!”

“She’s fine.” The ebony monkey tossed back easily. And sure enough the young raven did emerge from the water with a smile.

“MK avenge me!”

“Now hold on-” The dark monkey was cut off as he felt MK collide with him and begin to try and grapple him.

“Get him!” Wukong cheered from the safety of the shore.

They all played in the water till exhaustion started to creep in, at least for Macaque; Bai He and MK seemed to have an endless supply of energy as they continued their fun. The dark monkey joined Wukong in laying out the lunch they had packed for the day.

“I already set out the loungers.” Wukong tilted his head indicating where indeed two lounge chairs were set up ready and waiting; one even had an umbrella providing shade for whoever laid underneath it.

Macaque’s heart fluttered at the gesture of Wukong remembering he did not enjoy sunbathing as often as Wukong did. Yet he quickly stamped it down. He didn’t want to taint today with the complications of his feel- his thoughts on Wukong.

So to distract himself he began to unpack the food he had helped Pigsy pre-make. Pigsy had done most the heavy lifting but there were some dishes he was sure Bai He would recognize as his own creations.

“What is this!” Wukong exclaimed in an excited squeal before bodily shoving Macaque aside. The dark monkey let out an indigent “Hey” as he flailed his arms to retain his balance.

Wukong, meanwhile, picked up an apple slice carved into the shape of a rabbit and coo-ed over it. The dark monkey quirked an eyebrow at Wukong’s praise but then his attention turned towards the sound of his children approaching.

“Yeah food!” MK cheered as he bee-lined it towards the two monkey demons. Bai He followed slowly after.

“Guys look at this!” Wukong held up the apple slice he was admiring as if it was the greatest treasure in all the world. “Isn’t it adorable!”

“Aw!” MK also coo-ed over the treat and seeing a whole plate of the apple bunnies, he picked one up for himself. “They’re almost too cute to eat!”

“I can’t believe Pigsy’s been holding out on us all these years!” Wukong complained, making Macaque chuckle.

“Uh Wukong-”

“Baba, you made my bunny snacks!” The young girl cheered as she broke into a run, now being close enough to see what the golden monkey was marveling over.

“Wha- You made these Baba?!” MK whipped his head towards the dark monkey who now grabbed a bunny snack and held it out to Bai He.

“Yup.” He popped the P and combed the young girls wet raven locks back so they wouldn’t be in her face. “They are some of Bai He’s favorite.”

“I used to love eating them piece by piece.” Bai He explained and then as if to prove her point she raised the bunny snack to her lips and bit through the head part.

“You made these?” Wukong asked, as he looked from Macaque to the cute apple slices carved to look like bunnies.

Macaque smirked with pride as he nodded and grabbed his own bunny snack. He then copied his daughter in biting off its head.

“See how these guys have jam noses and black berry bits for eyes?” Bai He explained as she pointed at MK and Wukong’s snacks.

The apple slices not only were carved to look like bunnies but also had black eyes (pieces of black berries) and where the nose would be was some jam dabbed on the tip. On the tray the bunny slices were on were baby carrots and some shredded apple bits.

“Sometimes Baba will use raspberries instead and will peel the whole apple and I’d call them my albino bunnies!” She explained with a smile as she finished off her bunny snack and went to grab another.

“Wait! Did you also make these little octopuses?” Wukong interjected and pointed down to the little hot dogs and sausages that had 8 legs that curled upwards and had smiles on their faces.

“Octopi” Macaque corrected but nodded yes.

“Those are so cute!” MK cheered as he immediately grabbed one and some of the chips that surrounded the little treats.

“I like to eat each leg one by one.” Bai He said to MK who looked mildly disturbed by his sisters seemingly evil eating habits.

“You bite off the heads of all the animal crackers don’t you?” MK asked with squinted eyes.

The young girl clicked her teeth at her brother in response. The two then broke into laughter and Macaque gave a chuckle as he grabbed some food of his own.

“When did you learn to make this stuff?” Wukong asked in an odd tone as he too loaded his own plate with food.

The dark monkey hmm-ed as he took a seat at the picnic table. With his tail he gestured over to Bai He who was still grabbing some carrots to accompany her bunny snacks.

“Had to do something to get her to eat.” Macaque explained in a tone plagued with memories of difficult dinners and lunches. “You got to keep the five star chef,” At this point Bai He had sat down next to Wukong and MK next to Macaque. Both kids quietly eating their food and listening, while Wukong and Macaque’s food was untouched. “But for us without a gourmet cook I had to be creative in getting her to eat”

“You are so lucky.” Bai He said to MK, who seemed to preen at the compliment given to his butler/chef.

“I don’t think I’d call Pigsy’s food gourmet.” Wukong tried to argue but was met with flat, unimpressed stares from Macaque and Bai He; MK looked offended.

“Dad, we had lobster thermidor the other day.” Bai He stressed. “You know what we normally have for dinner?” She gave him an expectant look to which Wukong was only able to cautiously open his mouth before she answered her own question with indignation. “Spaghetti! Unless we go out for food!”

Macaque quickly bit into the sandwich he picked up to hide the hurt from Bai He’s comment. He invited the teasing but it didn’t feel good to hear his daughter rave about Wukong’s home and put down their own.

“Again you are so lucky!” She said to MK who nodded in agreement.

“Yeah. I just wish he’d let me go out for food more.” MK said then looked between Bai He and Macaque. “You guys being here has probably been the most I’ve been able to eat out. Other than crashing at Mei’s.”

“Yeah we were even able to order pizza!” Wukong chimed in with a smile. “Can’t remember the last time I was able to get away with that at home.”

“Oh I feel so bad for you both.” Bai He said sarcastically as she chomped into a carrot.

“Does Tang not cook?” The golden monkey asked as he grabbed some peach chips. “That guy seems to know good food.”

Macaque and Bai He snorted and then broke into laughter.

“Absolutely not!”

“He almost burned down our complex!” Bai He exaggerated. “He can’t be trusted to boil water!”

“Not unsupervised.” Macaque added, causing everyone to laugh. “Tang appreciates good food, and has great taste, but no he can’t cook.” The ebony monkey explained. “Though he does do pretty good at helping me carve the smiles into the octopi.”

MK then asked if Macaque could show him how to make the octopi and bunny snacks when they get home, to which his Baba agreed. And then the conversation changed to a few different topics till finally everyone had finished their lunches.

Bai He and MK went back to the water, eager to continue to play in the sand and surf. Meanwhile Macaque and Wukong both went to lay out on the lounge chairs Wukong had previously set up. For a while the two lounged in a comfortable silence, both allowing their lunches to be digested as they listened and watched their kids play. They would throw a passing comment out there as they observed MK doing something dangerous or Bai He be oblivious to an incoming wave. But for the most part it was quiet.

Wukong was never one for silences, even if it was comfortable. So he turned to start up a conversation with Macaque and was startled to find the ebony monkey fast asleep. He boggled for a second, since last time he glanced over to the darker monkey he was awake. So the golden monkey just stared at Macaque for some time, seeing if the six-eared monkey was faking it. However the steady breathing and peaceful look on Macaque’s face confirmed it; he was out.

Wukong scratched his head at that. Sure Mac seemed tired but the golden monkey didn’t think he’d pass out that quickly. He could get it he supposed, the atmosphere was perfect for a quick nap. The weather was ideal, the sound of the kids' laughter was comforting, and with a stomach full of food…

Wukong stretched and decided he would follow in Macaque’s footsteps. He fully relaxed in his lounger, arms folded behind his head, and feet stretched out. He let his tail hang out more in the shade and buried the tip of his tail in the sand, as a mini tail-icure. However as he settled in and fully relaxed, he startled with a jolt as he felt Mac’s tail wrap around his own. The golden monkey stilled and glanced over to Mac to see he was still asleep but his tail had unconsciously encircled his own.

Wukong knew he should pull his tail away. It wasn’t wrapped so tightly. It wasn’t entrapped. So he should pull away…but what if that woke Macaque? Wukong glanced at the ebony monkeys face again and even though he could only see his profile he knew the ebony monkey probably still frowned as he napped. He always frowned in his sleep…except no he didn’t.

During their honeymoon, back in their one bedroom apartment, when they first started the adoption process, and in the days before all that when they were much younger. Macaque didn’t sleep with a frown. But with life came stress; and Macaque seemed like he could never let the stressers go, even in his sleep. Wukong would tease him about it…Did he ever offer to help with it? He couldn't remember. He’s sure he did offer to help release some of the weight Mac always carried. He just couldn’t remember, he was also busy and…

…Wukong didn’t need to pull his tail away. It’s not like holding tails is cheating. But it wasn’t something Wukong would do in front of Azure…or tell him about.

Silently Wukong raked a hand down his face in frustration. ’Yeah this isn’t good.’ Wukong thought. And before his internal battle could continue, Mac’s tail pulled away.

The golden monkey whipped his head over to see what happened and saw Macaque had rolled onto his side, his back now facing Wukong. His tail had wrapped itself around his waist, in a semblance of giving himself a hug.

Wukong’s own tail twitched in Mac’s direction before he put an effort into stopping it. Giving himself a shake Wukong readjusted himself ‘Well’ He thought, ‘That takes care of that.’ Wukong closed his eyes again, willing himself to fall asleep. However he opened his eyes and looked back at Mac’s back to him with a frown. He then shook his head again and tried to pass out, willing his mind to go blank and allow himself to fall asleep.

 

Wukong awoke to a volley ball nailing him in the face. With a loud “Oof!” and exaggerated flailing the golden monkey jerked sideways, tumbling off the lounger and into the sand.

Macaque jolted up from the noise and immediately began asking “What happened?!” as he scanned the beach for any signs of danger.

“Sorry Dad!” MK yelled from the volleyball court.

Chapter End Notes

I wonder if there’s any foreshadowing in this chapter??? >.>

Sorry for the wait guys, but I actually had a real hard time fluffing this chapter up. I wanted family beach bonding and then my mind just blanked out and I couldn’t write it out properly. I still think it’s a bit stiff but I’m happy enough with it. :)

Chapter 10: Cat Cafe Interlude

Chapter Notes

See the end of the chapter for notes

Pigsy and Tang sat at a table in Sandy’s cat cafe. Tang was petting a friendly tabby that wondered near him, while Pigsy gently shoo-ed a cat away from them.

“Aw Pigsy, have a heart.” Tang teased earning only a roll of the eyes from said pig demon.

“That one loves to jump on tables. That’s right Mittens I’m talking about ya!” Pigsy pointed at the cat that looked offended at being called out.

“We’re still working on breaking her of that habit.” Sandy added as he continued to clean the underside of the empty tables and chairs.

The cafe was closed but Sandy never denied his friends a visit and feline companionship. So when a huffy Pigsy and slightly bewildered Tang arrived at his doorstep he took it as an opportunity to do some deeper cleaning.

“We?” Tang questioned.

“Me and Mo!” Sandy pointed at a blue cat with a mohawk sitting prominently on the cafe’s counter, right by the cash register.

Tang shared a look with Pigsy who just shook his head. “Just nod along.” the demon advised.

“Ok…”

A beat of silence passed as Pigsy took a long slow pull of his drink. Tang was still absently petting the tabby but began to feel the tingles of awkwardness creeping in. Afterall he had been pulled away and out of the house by Pigsy and all but pushed into this cafe. No explanation but a gruff and tense “I need to be away from them.” sentence as an explanation.

“Pretty close to dinner time.” Sandy noted with no set tone, but it had the vibe of being pointed. And from Pigsy’s shackles visibly rising, it seems it did not miss its mark.

“I made the food and they can serve themselves!” Pigsy barked to which Sandy only blinked innocently at.

“Pigsy, did something happen? I thought everything was great today.” Tang asked.

The day did seem genuinely great, at least from his point of view. MK and Macaque had spent a lot of the morning together in the gym. Bai He, Wukong and Azure had been poolside most the day with Tang dropping in occasionally to check in on the young girl. There was some weirdness with MK and Azure, the bulky blue lion seemed to almost be babystepping his way around the teenager. It wasn’t obvious but Tang was good at catching subtle behavior in people. Most people forget about Tang’s presence so he gets almost a birds eye view of people, and Azure was no exception.

Pigsy slammed his cup down in anger and immediately blurts out the cause of his frustration “I draw the line when Bai He tells me I have to be nicer to that-” The pig demon cut himself off with a growl.

“Have you not been civil-” Sandy tried to ask.

“I’ve been fine!” Pigsy cut the blue bulky man off with a glare, but under the giant's patient gaze his glare withered. Till he grumbled into his cup and took another sip.

“Ah...yes, things have been tense in that department.” Tang added as he patted the still frustrated pig demon's arm.

“How dare he get to her!” Pigsy growls. “Wukong I can get, but Bai He?!”

“Wait, what do you mean get to her?” Tang questioned, his posture becoming rigid and the tabby he was previously petting leapt off his lap. No longer satisfied with his now stiff body posture.

“I mean he’s got her convinced he’s somehow the victim in all this!”

“Victim?” Tang now felt lost. He glanced at Sandy for some help but the blue demon had his face twisted in concern.

“It’s what he does!” Pigsy continued angrily. “No matter the situation, somehow it’s never his fault or he’s not to blame at all, and he’s always the victim!” The Pig demon threw his hands up in the air in frustration. “Wukong blames himself all the time so as soon as I recognized the signs on Azure shifting blame I called him out on it, but did Wukong listen?!” Tang and Sandy shook their heads in the negative as Pigsy blurted out “Of course not!” He gripped his head in frustration. “I planned on warning the kids but then everything was happening at once and I didn’t want to put more on them. Poor things have been through such a roller coaster already. But of course the pushy bitch couldn’t listen to what Wukong wanted!” He slammed his hands down on the table. “Cause he’s a selfish asshole but Wukong refuses to see it! And now he’s got Bai He under his thumb!”

“I don’t know if she’s under his thumb.” Tang reasoned. “She’s a very sensitive girl. She probably just gravitated over to him since he is the odd man out.”

Sandy threw in a word of agreement but the burly pig waved them off. “No, that asshole got to her. I can tell! We just have to keep an eye on MK.”

“Then why are we here?”

“Because a change of scenery is a great way to balance out our moods.” Sandy answered as he approached the two, placing a new cup of tea down in front of Pigsy. “Sometimes it’s best to remove yourself from the area of stress. It can encourage our brains to break from routine thought patterns.”

“Huh.” Tang nodded at the information then turned back to the pig demon who finished up his first cup before grabbing the new cup Sandy had provided.

“So how’d you end up working for Macaque?” Pigsy asked, abruptly changing the subject; and shocking Tang at the unexpected question.

“Oh, yes I’m curious to learn that as well!” Sandy smiled and immediately grabbed a spare chair to pull it over so he could sit with the duo.

“Oh!” Tang adjusted his glasses and took a sip of his tea. “Well actually we started off more as roommates then transitioned into me taking on more of a caretaker role.”

“Roommates?” Pigsy questioned.

“Yes sir.” Tang then took on a more confident stature and continued to tell of his and his bosses past.

“You see, I'm actually a historian by trade. I used to do history lectures at the City College of New York.” He looked particularly prideful at this and continued. “I was always working on preserving the historical monuments, sites, and buildings all around New York. So frequently I would be in City Hall, advocating for these historic monuments. I saw Macaque there several times. Never knew what he was going there for. But being a frequent visitor I had made note of most of the regulars and Macaque was one of them.” He then looked sheepish for a second and rubbed the back of his head. “Honestly I thought he was a clerk or secretary.”

“Anyway we got to talking and he was able to get me a meeting with the Mayor of New York. With the Mayor’s help, a building I was advocating for would be refurbished instead of torn down.” He beamed at the memory. “It’s still there today!”

“Wow!” Sandy praised.

“So, what? You guys just moved in together after that?”

“Sort of.” Tang shrugged. “I would usually watch Bai He for him. Eventually it got to a point where my apartment lease was going to expire so we decided to just move in together.” He shrugged again. “Then his career was really taking off and I was needing to watch Bai He so much it just made sense to quit my teaching job and become a full time butler.”

 

In truth Macaque and Tang’s beginning was a bit more involved than that.

Tang can still remember the day Macaque had brought Bai He to City Hall, still a baby in her infant car seat. The dark monkey had looked murderous as he talked animatedly with the ever smiling Mayor, so Tang couldn’t help but move closer in curiosity. Macaque had said he didn’t have anyone to watch his daughter so their meeting had to be canceled but the Mayor said she’d have to attend their meeting as well since he wouldn’t let the dark monkey cancel it. This offer made the ebony monkey look ready to pounce and physically assault the smiling man.

To this day Tang couldn’t say what caused him to intervene. Maybe it was his inner hero finally taking the stage, or maybe it was the cute baby that looked at him from her place in the car seat. But intervene he did. He cut in and offered to watch the baby while they had their meeting.

“I swear I’m not a weirdo. I’m a Professor and I’ve seen you here several times! I’m sure you didn’t notice me but-”

“I did.” Macaque had cut him off.

“Huh?”

“You’re the loud one. You talk a lot.” He explained with an unimpressed face.

“Uh, yes- well the monuments and buildings I represent require explanations most of the time so I-”

“So it’s settled then!” The Mayor had cut in.

Macaque had threatened Tang up and down, which the glasses wearing man took in stride as he was by all accounts a stranger (a familiar stranger, but a stranger). Tang had given Macaque his wallet in a show of good faith, which seemed to ease the demon monkey somewhat. The monkey had given him the diaper bag and told him they’d be done in 15 minutes to which the Mayor corrected it would be 30. Either way, not too long a time to watch a baby while sitting in City Hall.

Macaque had rushed back to them once he and the Mayor were done and seemed surprised at how well the young baby was with Tang. And the babysitting adventures continued from there. He had been watching Bai He for so long and was over so often it just made sense for their next apartment to be a 3 bedroom and split the rent. And then it progressed from there.

 

“And you were fine with leaving your career?” Pigsy asked, genuinely curious. As he looked at Tang in a new light. A man that gave up what was clearly his passion in order to help raise a baby.

Tang smiled easily “I missed it for some time; but I did do some guest lectures and night classes. But honestly,” He leaned back and took a long sip of his drink. “I prefer being a butler. We have a cleaning service, we order in most of our food, and Bai He’s at school a lot-”

“Wait a minute, what do you even do?!” Pigsy cut in with an exasperated tone, his new image of Tang tainting with the fresher image of a man laying around watching TV.

“I provide wit, wisdom, and care to Bai He and Macaque.”

Sandy laughed as Pigsy’s head thunked against the table.

“What about you guys?” Tang asked, “How did you all meet Wukong?”

“I met Wukong through Pigsy!” Sandy answered readily. Tang stared at him waiting for more elaboration but Sandy only looked politely back.

“Oh I see.” Tang finally responded with a strained smile, realizing no further elaboration would be coming. He then turned to the pig demon. “What about you Pigsy?”

Said demon drew in a long breath and then let it out in a weary sigh. “I needed a job.” He let out a huff as the memories played in the back of his mind. “I always wanted to open my own restaurant but money was tight. Took me a lot of budgeting and borrowing for me to be able to finish my culinary school. Then I kept getting fired from these so-called high class restaurants!”

“What? Why?”

“Some were just racist against demons or pig demons in particular at the one joint.” He waved those memories away. “But others, fired me cause I get a bit heated about food.”

“I’ve noticed.” Tang drawled and Sandy shared an amused side eye with the human.

“But it’s basic 101 kitchens need to be clean! And I’d complained about being the only one cleaning all those dumps, and they’d say just leave it as good enough! Good enough?! We’re serving people food! Cleanliness matters! And ingredients! Don’t even get me started!”

Pigsy continued to rant about some of his old jobs and Sandy worried all this ranting and negativity would bring down Tang or deter the man. However when the blue demon glanced at the human he was surprised to see the dark haired man enraptured. He smiled at some insults Pigsy said about the restaurants and looked offended at some revelations about places he previously heard good things about. Sandy smiled to himself as he looked between Tang and Pigsy.

“So is that when you met Wukong?”

“No, actually I met Wukong and Macaque through Tripitaka.”

“Wukong’s manager?” Tang tilted his head in confusion.
