Chapter 13: The Plan

Chapter Notes

So I'll go into this in more detail when I post my final chapter for this fic but I just want to thank all of you for your comments and encouragements after Chapter 12, I really needed it and I can't even tell you how much I appreciated it!

I also want to give a special shout out to the repeat commenters from before Chapter 12. And again I'll go into more detail on this later but you guys were the whole reason I even posted Chapter 12. So thank you all so much! You gave me the drive to post the last chapter which then all of you like exploded over and that got me going :DDD So I will give a special shout out to you all in the final chapter (because yes I have a counter, don't judge me lol) but I did want to do a private thanks for right now.

Repeaters and new commenters welcome and THANK YOU ALL so much for clicking on this fic and reading it :DDD

It was late into the night and yet MK and Bai He couldn’t sleep. They were both in the main family room, watching random TV shows. Though neither of them were actually watching what was playing, they were just trying to dull themselves to what had happened hours prior.

Blinking out of his stupor MK glanced at his sister who was subdued and clearly lost in her own thoughts like he had just been. With a sigh of defeat MK turned the TV off. That seemed to rouse Bai He and after a few blinks she glanced at MK who gestured with his head to follow him.

“I got an idea.” He whispered and then they quietly walked downstairs.

Their Dad had gone to bed earlier, gently asking the kids if they had needed anything. After being told no he had bid them good night and asked them to try to not stay up too late. Before leaving he did hesitate and with a tone riddled with guilt, told them that he would take care of the paparazzi and they were not to worry about that or school or anything. MK was tempted to go after him, but he honestly didn’t know what he could say. Pigsy had tried calling Sandy but the blue giant had already promised he would be at the trauma center today. He offered to come at night but both MK and Bai He politely declined. Sandy assured them he’d be there tomorrow.

Pigsy and Tang had disappeared some time ago; before they had sat in the room with the kids. Pigsy plied them with food and water while Tang wrapped them in a blanket. MK and Bai He weren’t sure where they had gone or how long they had been gone for.

MK guided Bai He towards the workout room. Thinking they could exhaust themselves into quieting their minds and passing out. However, Bai He stopped him and pointed back at the door they had just passed. It was to the wine cellar but it had light streaming through the crack of the door that hadn’t fully closed.

With a quirk of an eyebrow MK shrugged and went to verbally respond when Bai He held a finger to her lips. Her face was serious as she gestured for him to remain quiet.

Quietly walking back to the door, Bai He opened it slowly so as to not make any noise. She then gestured for MK to follow her. The young man was confused but went along with his sister. They quietly crept into the wine room but stopped upon hearing Tang and Pigsy. Bai He gestured for them to stay low and MK was really confused as to why they were now spying on their butlers.

“I swear I didn’t lie about my feelings!” Tang practically yelled, and MK flinched at the suddenness of it. Bai He’s eyes narrowed as she leaned closer to try and hear better as Tang continued. “What do I have to do to get you to believe me?!”

“Hey! I said to keep it down!” Pigsy barked. “Don’t want to wake the kids.” The pig demon sighed “If they can get any sleep after the shit show that happened.” It sounded like the pig demon was glaring at Tang when saying that.

“I know it was bad!” Tang sounded defensive but did bring his volume back to a more normal level.

“You said that already.”

Tang let out a sigh and after a beat of silence continued. “I do like you! A lot! Every word I said was the truth!”

MK looked confused and glanced over to Bai He, but that was no help. Bai He looked vindicated, as if she was just proven right about something.

“Yeah?” Pigsy challenged, “Then start being truthful about everything.”

“...I told you I can’t say.” Tang sighed and it sounded like Pigsy either walked away or threw his hands up, MK wasn’t sure which.

“Then how can you expect me to believe-?!”

“Ok, ok!” Tand rushed. “I…I can’t explain it all but I can…” The human made sounds of thought before continuing “I can give you the synopsis.”

“The synop-? Would you just be clear and tell me everything!” Pigsy sounded exasperated, and frankly MK was there with him; and he’s only listened in for maybe a minute now.

“You see,” Tang began in a tone of one telling a grand tale; MK immediately leaned closer as did Bai He. “I do like you. Very much! I was hoping to continue to build our relationship and hopefully have it naturally progress. Though if we didn’t go on one official date before summer ended, then I was going to ask Macaque to help me kidnap you.” Pigsy made a startled sound and both kids had to put hands over their mouths to hold back their laughter. “But…remember how you told me you didn’t trust Azure?” MK shared a look with Bai He, both looked confused at that.

“Yeah?”

“You are right not to.”

“What did he do?” Pigsy’s tone was deadly serious.

“This is the part I can’t get too much into.” Tang hedged.

“But he did something?”

“...Yes.” Bai He and MK both gasped but the adults must not have heard them as they kept going. “And that’s why I had to make a very public confession.”

“How does that-?!”

“So it would make sense for me to stay here and watch the kids around him.” Tang cut in, and MK’s mind was a buzz.

“Did Baba come up with that plan?”

MK jolted bodily and looked over to where Bai He was, except now he only saw empty air. ‘Damn she is like a ninja!’ MK thought as he bolted upright and saw the young girl had rounded the furniture they were hiding behind. He quickly joined her and took in the shock and horror on the adults face.

“You were not supposed to hear that!” Tang wheezed, looking pale and like he wished the floor would eat him.

“What are you kids doing up?! It’s past 1 am!” Pigsy reprimanded. “I thought you two had passed out by now!”

“We couldn’t sleep.” MK shrugged and the pig demon sighed and slumped in defeat.

“Can’t say I blame you for that.”

“I knew something was wrong.” Bai He said, her gaze never leaving Tang. “The more and more I thought about it. The more I knew something was wrong!”

“Bai He-” Tang tried to gently approach the young girl but she stepped back.

“I knew Baba wouldn’t just leave me here, he barely wanted me to go to camp. And you!” She pointed at her butler. “You would never be that nervous or make such a weird confession. I’ve seen you hit on people and you never did anything close whatever that was!” She gestured widely between Tang and Pigsy.

Pigsy grunted and folded his arms, seemingly in agreement of Tang’s confession being bogus. MK looked at Tang’s face of rising panic and decided to help the man out.

“Hey now I think he was being genuine!” Tang shot MK a relieved and happy look while Pigsy only raised an eyebrow and still looked doubtful. “I mean we could all see you two had…” He gestured between the two adults, who were now both blushing. “Something! Honestly, I thought you guys had already gone out on a few dates.” He looked to Bai He but she shook her head in the negative. “Well maybe it was just me then.” He shrugged. “But he just explained that he does like you Pigsy, he just had to rush things because….because of Azure?” MK’s tone ended in a question as he looked back towards the adult human. Next to him Bai He folded her arms and set a glare on Tang.

“What’s going on?” She demanded.

“I…I can’t tell you.” All three let out groans so Tang rushed to placate them. “B-But I can tell you a bit!”

“We need the truth, the whole truth.” Pigsy rebuffed. “These kids deserve to know why Macaque abandoned them!”

MK flinched at that word and Bai He squeezed herself tighter. Tang quickly rounded on the pig demon with a glare of his own, and Pigsy grumbled in remorse but didn’t back down.

“He didn’t abandon them!” He defended vehemently. “Azure…you can’t let on that you know!” Tang said sternly. He then looked towards all three of them and looked each of them in the eye. “Macaque didn’t even want to tell me! He definitely doesn't want you guys to know anything about this!” All three nodded and gave affirmations of keeping their silence. “Azure’s blackmailing Macaque.” Tang finally admitted to the three.

“With what?” Bai He and MK asked.

“What for?” Pigsy inquired, putting an arm out to gesture to the kids to hold back on that.

“He gave Macaque a day to go back to New York and leave Bai He and MK here.”

“So that’s why he left!” MK breathed in relief, the knowledge that his Baba had not abandoned him taking a huge weight off his heart. He turned to Bai He to share in this feeling, as he expected to see her also relieved. Yet the young girl just looked upset, tears quickly gathering in her eyes.

“Why didn’t he tell us?! Tell me! He never talks to me!” Bai He ranted with tears leaking out of the corners of her eyes. MK and Tang reached out to comfort her but she backed away from them. “I said such mean things! I was so mad and-and hurt!” Bei He cried. “I thought Azure was my-my friend.” She hiccupped and rubbed at her eyes. “Bu-but he…he did this! I-I…I just-!”

“Hey, hey!”

MK forcefully pulled the now sobbing girl into a tight embrace. He pet her head to comfort her, and made soothing sounds. He then jolted when he felt himself and Bai He both being hugged by Pigsy and Tang. Once Bai He’s sobs subsided the group hug fell away, though MK kept a hand on the young girls shoulder, to reassure her he was there.

Pigsy was the one to break the ensuing silence “Listen, your Baba’s tuff. He knows you love him.” He assured with a gentle smile, then gave a soft glare to Tang. “And yeah he should have just told us-”

“No.” MK cut in, startling everyone. However MK stood firm in his words. “No he was right not to.” Pigsy looked offended and confused, while Bai He tilted her head in confusion. “Don’t get me wrong!” MK quickly added, holding a hand up “That was not fun! And I’ll definitely need to talk with Sandy about a lot in the near future!” He flashed a smile which loosened his sister and butler up. “But,” his tone became serious again. “If Azure was blackmailing him then it had to be a big dramatic exit. That’s what Azure wanted. I mean he was the one to tell me to look for Baba when you guys were loading the bags by the door! And then” He pointed at Pigsy. “You and him arrived shortly after me. That can’t be a coincidence.”

“Yeah,” Pigsy scratched his chin. “He said he heard yelling and suggested we check it out.”

MK nodded his head. “He wanted a dramatic exit. And if Baba had told us he was being blackmailed, well then Azure would probably just spill the beans then and there, and make his drama that way!” The three nodded their heads at MK’s evaluation, though Bai He still looked upset and Pigsy still looked miffed.

“I still think it could have been handled better.” Pigsy grumbled.

“It was a plan thought up in less than an hour, give us some credit.” Tang chided and poked the pig demon who batted at his finger with a huff.

Silence followed as everyone digested all the information that was shared. The silence was then broken by a loud and long groan.

“Macaque’s going to kill me.” Tang anguished. “He hasn’t even landed in New York yet and I already did the one thing he said not to!” He dragged his hands down his face in misery “When he calls in the morning you all have to act like you don’t know!”

“Wait!” Bai He startled. “What’s the next step?! Baba always plans things out, so what’s next?” She grabbed Tang and pulled on his clothes in her urgency for an answer.

“Well…” Tang scratched the side of his face, his expression unsure. “Macaque said he was going to go digging.”

“And get his own blackmail! Genius!” MK declared with enthusiasm.

“No, it’s not!” Pigsy broke in, his angry expression back on his face. “That’s really his whole plan? Get blackmail to offset the blackmail Azure has on him? What then huh?! They just have a showdown of who’s dirt is worse?!”

Tang gave a helpless shrug as an answer.

“It would work if we had it.” Bai He said, her expression far off as she thought about everything. She then tapped her chin with one finger, “If MK and I had blackmail on Azure we could use it.”

“Wait-” Tang tried but was cut off.

“Yeah!” MK agreed quickly. “We’ll be playing dumb to him blackmailing Baba anyway, so he won’t suspect it!”

MK and Bai He beamed at each other and began making a game plan. Pigsy and Tang shared a nervous look and Pigsy rubbed his forehead.

“This is not a good idea.”

-

This was turning into the longest summer of Azure’s life. He still couldn’t believe all that was happening, while his end goal was in sight! All the work, research, build up! Everything had been worth it as he could see the endless amount of open doors just in his purview. But he was getting impatient. It was understandable he thought, after all it had taken him so long to even get to where he was now.

He remembered when he first met Wukong. Wukong was up and coming and everyone could see he was on the path to great success. He had great charisma, was attractive, and was talented. So naturally he had befriended the monkey demon as Azure wanted to keep good relationships with all the actors and actresses that had talent. But suddenly doors that he had to fight to even get near were opened for him. And that was just by being Wukong’s friend!

Yet Azure, like his friend and brother Peng, seemed to fall into typecasting. Where Peng always played the villain or antagonist; Azure was always cast as the love interest. The wholesome man that melted their partners heart or helped redeem them. Or he was the ex that got another chance, a reconnection. And then in others he was what he was playing now, the better boyfriend.

Doors opened by being Wukong’s friend but there was a whole world of connections, industries, and people constantly watching the golden monkey. And they only watched Wukong. Being Wukong’s friend did not get him out of his typecast, didn’t get him into deals with companies, didn’t get him as noticed as some adopted teenager who just walks beside the golden monkey. Wukong’s child has been on more Magazine covers than Azure ever has! And so he and his brothers Peng and Yellow Tusk came up with an idea. If Azure could get into a relationship with Wukong he could use his popularity to launch all of them higher. He could bury their past with all the connections at his disposal. He had the skills, charm, and a foot in the door with his friendship.

It had taken some time. They had watched Wukong, dug into his past relationship history, tracked down some of the people who Wukong had brief flings with and interviewed them extensively. The research and payoffs had all been worth it though since at the end they had figured out what type of boyfriend would be best. Then it was up to Azure’s acting and wit to catch the golden monkey.

And it had not been quick. Wukong had walls up in regards to relationships. He seemed fine with flings but was cautious and took a lot of Azure prodding and subtle manipulation in order for Wukong to even consider it. Then things had been going well. Wukong had wanted Azure to meet MK and once he had the kids' approval then he knew nothing could stop their relationship from becoming official. Peng had wanted to leak it before, but Azure knew he needed to get the kids approval, which he was confident in acquiring. He could not have known about Bai He and Macaque!

They were so close and it had felt like robbery that Macaque had just re-appeared like this. Azure was so tempted to go ahead and leak their relationship but knew that risked Macaque’s presence getting noticed. Which would then bring all focus to Macaque and Wukong. He could see the headlines “Wukong and Macaque reconnecting?” “Not so divorced?!”. He would not allow it, and he would not allow all his work to go up in smoke!

So it was with hidden glee that he watched Macaque depart the mansion. When Wukong had come back inside, looking like a shell of himself, Azure offered him a hand and shoulder to lean on.

That night he toasted with his brothers. For tomorrow he would talk with Wukong about officially announcing their relationship to the world. He already had his battle plan in place. He’d mention how it would divert attention from the kids and if that didn’t work he’d use the guilt card. It always worked a charm on Wukong, the man had a guilt complex that was easy for Azure to use to his advantage.

Chapter 14: Getting Help

Chapter Notes

Day 1 after Macaque's departure.

See the end of the chapter for more notes

Wukong ran his hand through his hair for the fifth time in the past half hour. In front of him were various papers. There were school pamphlets and FAQs brochures scattered on the table top. Then on a notebook piece of paper he had a list of items he needed to request from Bai He’s old school, at the bottom of the page was an angrily written “TANG” in all caps. On another piece of paper was Bai He’s old school’s name followed by various phone numbers with several crossed out and one circled several times with doodles of stars and various “success” logos.

Since Macaque had left so damn quickly he didn’t give Wukong anything! So he had asked his daughter for the school name and then began scouring the phone books for the schools phone number. Countless phone calls later he finally had the correct number. Only to then hit another roadblock of not being authorized to discuss Bai He’s school records. So he needed Tang to call them back and request the various items he would need.

Picking up one of the school pamphlets Wukong skimmed it again, trying to decide if it would be the best option. He needed somewhere discreet where no paparazzi would gain access and where his relation to her wouldn’t be leaked. Yet as he scanned the pamphlets his mind spiraled about how to finagle that and how to make drop offs and pickups work. And eventually he slammed the paper down and grabbed his mouth to let out a scream of frustration into his palm.

“There you are.” Azure greeted joyfully as he came up behind the visibly agitated monkey demon. “Oh baby.” He cooed as he grabbed Wukong’s shoulders and began kneading them. “You’re so tense.”

“Of course I’m tense!” Wukong snapped, slamming his hands on the table. “I can’t believe he just left like that!”

“I know.” Azure intoned with sympathy.

“And to leave Bai He here without even talking to me?!” Wukong scoffed. “The asshole!”

“Well I think her being here is ultimately-”

“I’m happy to have her here!” Wukong cut off, jerking his body away from Azure and moving to stand up. “That’s not what I meant! But he didn’t talk with me at all! And now I have to deal with all this!” He gestured wildly to the cluttered desk. “I have to figure out her schooling, and then the press! And I have to take care of MK and the press following him!” He groaned, his shoulders hunched as he felt the pressure on him mount and mount.

“You know what will help?” Azure asked patiently. “Thinking about something else! Let’s talk about something fun for right now. Take a break from all this.” He pointed at the desk.

“Azure.” Wukong sighed, still clearly frustrated.

“Let’s plan how we announce our relationship! That will be fun!” The lion demon smiled and tried to grab Wukong’s hand again. “And it will take the spotlight away from the kids, which I think is really-”

“Azure I don’t have time to think about that right now! I’m on a time crunch!” Wukong said as he stepped away from the lion demon. “I have all this to-”

“Wukong we’ve put this off for our whole relationship!” Azure whined and closed his eyes as he grabbed the spot over his heart. “I understood waiting till I met MK but now it really feels like you’re just ashamed of me.” Azure opened his eyes and looked pleadingly at Wukong.

However the golden monkey’s shoulders only rose in agitation. “Re-really?!” He snapped and Azure’s eyes widened at Wukong combating him. “Azure I’m not ashamed of you. I just don’t have time right now!” He pointed harshly at his desk. “I have to get Bai He’s school records, pick a school, enroll her! Talk with the press about MK’s coverage! Change my travel plans for upcoming projects! I just-I..I-I have to take care of my kids! A media circus of anything is not good right now!”

“But!” Azure looked genuinely panicked

“I’ll be back later.” Wukong cut him off with a shake of his head. Grabbing the two notebook pieces of paper, he then briskly walked past the stunned lion demon.

Azure stared at Wukong’s retreating back as he stood stock still in the celebrity’s office. This was the first time his guilt inducing missed its mark on Wukong. But what really worried the lion demon, and what made him race back to his brothers, was what the super star had said: A media circus of anything is not good right now.

Once again, Peng had suggested to just leak the relationship.

“If we crack the door open then he’ll have to deal with the following flood.” Peng reasoned. “So we sell the story, let it get picked up and then once it’s out there -”

“Wukong could deny it.” Yellow Tusk cut in, his tone one of chastising. “Distance himself from Azure to minimize the rumor mill and let it die. And then if it is officially announced it won’t gain as much traction in case it’s another rumor.”

Azure’s head swiveled over towards his larger brother. “What do you mean if it’s announced?” He questioned harshly.

Peng also looked at the elephant demon with an eyebrow raised. Yet Yellow Tusk only folded his arms and gave the two a serious look. Then focusing on Azure he explained “Macaque leaving and Wukong wanting to seclude himself and his family, does not bode well for your relationship.”

“Pushing Macaque to leave was for the best! I told you they were falling back into old patterns! I couldn’t risk him being too close. ” Azure defended immediately. “Now MK and Bai He are against him and Wukong’s attention is back on me.” Azure then waved a hand in the air as if waving away a fly. “However, I agree we shouldn’t leak it.” Peng scoffed and folded their arms. “But we may need some leverage in case we need to force Wukong’s hand.”

“We’re never gonna find anything on Wukong. We’ve looked, in case you forgot.” Peng added.

“Not on Wukong,” Tusk muttered. “but we already have a good story to sell if Wukong pushes this off for too long.” He looked unhappy at the idea as he pondered it. “However, I'm hesitant to sell a story about a hidden daughter.”

“Plus it may dampen Wukong’s look, which will not help us.” Azure said as he rubbed his chin. “But it is the only back up option we have right now.”

“Wukong’s manager would just buy the story before it got published.” Peng added.

“That’s why we need to find more. Like Yellow Tusk said, we need information not on Wukong but people close to him, his kids, their friends, Pigsy.” He began to pace as he thought his plan through more and more. “If we get enough stories they won’t be able to just buy them all, but either offer a bigger story or cut the smaller stories off by getting a bigger story out there.”

“Assuming you can’t convince Wukong to announce your relationship. Which would still be the best option at this point.” Yellow Tusk said as he leaned back in his armchair.

“It never hurts to have dirt on people. Worked to get the ex out of here.” Peng chimed in. “So I say we do both.” They pointed at Azure. “You work on convincing the super star to go public while we,” They then gestured towards themself and Yellow Tusk “do some investigating.”

“Agreed.” Azure said while Yellow Tusk nodded.

-

Evidently MK was the last to wake up, which was surprising to him as they had all stayed up rather late the day before. Yet as he dragged himself into the kitchen he was greeted by the sight of an array of bagels, muffins, various egg dishes, and fruit arrangements. The fruit arrangements had clearly been raided by his Dad as all the peaches were gone and it looks like one banana was crushed, or something since there were bits of the fruit and peel around where one banana appeared to have previously been.

Tang was at the table looking as tired as MK felt, while Pigsy and Sandy were on the other side of the island in the kitchen both talking about the quality of some yogurt the blue giant had brought. Sitting on a stool with a bagel in one hand and pen in the other was Bai He. She appeared completely put together and was staring down at the paper she was writing on with a fierce intensity.

“Good Morning MK!” Sandy greeted upon noticing him. “Or almost afternoon I should say! Anyway, I brought some bagels and muffins for you all. Please have some!” He encouraged as he came around the island and physically lifted MK to place him on a stool next to Bai He.

“He also brought yogurt but we can’t have any.” Tang teased but then jumped in his seat at the glare the pig demon gave him.

“You’re already on thin ice, don’t make it worse!” Tang’s face fell as did MK’s in sympathy, yet Pigsy’s held firm and Bai He didn’t look away from her notes.

Sandy then went back to the other side of the island and leaned against the counter. “I know you two didn’t want me here yesterday night, but I’m here today for whatever you want.”

Sandy’s inviting atmosphere made MK’s shoulders relax at Sandy’s easy offer to talk. “Thanks Sandy.” He said with meaning and both men shared a smile before the blue giant looked to Bai He.

The young girl glanced up at Sandy then looked back at her notes. “We don’t need it anymore, we have a plan.”

“What?” All but Sandy asked the young girl who looked at all of them bewildered at their confusion.

“What?” She parroted back, frustration clear in her voice. “We don’t need to talk about this anymore. We have a plan. We need to focus on that!”

“Bai He, that doesn’t mean we don't have things we should talk out.” MK explained. “Yesterday was…a lot.”

“Yeah!” She slammed her hands on the island “Now imagine how it is on Baba?!” She fired back angry. “We have to focus on getting him back! We have to get stuff on Azure in order to get him back!” She glared at her brother.

“Sweetie,” Tang said to Bai He as he stood and put a hand on MK’s back in support; the poor boy sat ramrod straight at the lecture. “I know you want to bring Macaque back as soon-”

“Now!” She interrupted. “I want him back now!” her voice betrayed her as the anger cracked and desperation seeped in. “I don't want my Baba alone in New York!” She quickly wiped at the tears that gathered at the corner of her eyes. “We need to focus!” She then turned back to her notebook and glared at it, her pen once again clutched tightly in her hand.

MK remained ramrod straight as the guilt of not thinking of his Baba and just his own emotions washed over him. Bai He was right. Macaque was alone in New York and instead of trying to work as much as possible on getting him back, he wanted to sit here and have a talk about his feelings?! He could do that after! He may not even need it! Like Bai He said they do have a plan.

“What stuff do you need on Azure?” Sandy asked, breaking the silence that had fallen over all of them.

“...Azure’s blackmailing Baba.” MK answered when it seemed no one else was going to. “So Bai He and I are going to get something to blackmail him with and then…” MK rocked his head back and forth.

“And get him out of our lives!” Bai He finished sternly.

MK looked startled at Bai He’s announcement as last night they really didn’t talk too much about the fallout of their plan. He just wanted to make sure Macaque could come back. Yet it sounded like Bai He knew exactly what outcome she wanted.

“I see, well not fully.” Sandy amended with a gentle huff. “I only have half the story from last night.” He then turned to MK “Speaking of, MK you look half awake I think a good shower will help wake you up.”

“Oh,” MK rubbed the back of his head. “I’m really alright.”

“Then how about a dip in the pool? I can join you!” He leaned forward with an excited smile. “We can do some swim aerobics and exercise! I find that always helps get the-”

“On second thought!” MK interrupted. “I probably do need a shower!” He then quickly got up and made his way towards his bathroom.

“Oh! Another time then MK!” Sandy called after the retreating teenager. The water demon then turned back to Bai He who was glaring down at the pages of her notebook, angry they didn't’ have all the answers she desired.

“Well little miss, since MK’s gone and these two have their own things to discuss.” Sandy indicated his head towards Pigsy who scowled and Tang who let out a sigh. “Could you tell me about everything? You are the one with the plan,” He pointed at the notebook and Bai He blinked up at him, her glare withering away. “So I trust you aren’t hung up on.” He again passed a look to the two butlers. “Minor details.”

“Minor?!” Pigsy sputtered but Tang who had come around the island, tugged on his shirt before he could splutter on further.

“Can we talk? Please?” The glasses-wearing man asked.

Pigsy huffed but nodded and left, leaving only Bai He and Sandy alone.

“I hope they work it out.” Sandy sighed and Bai He nodded.

“Me too.” She then looked from her notebook to Sandy. “So what did Pigsy tell you?”

Sandy leaned away and against the far counter again, with a shrug he said. “Why don’t you tell me what happened? Pigsy gets passionate about some things and leaves out some other details.”

Nodding her head Bai He seemed to accept that answer. “Well Azure lied! He’s pretending to be a nice guy but he’s not! He blackmailed Baba into leaving me here! He made him leave!”

“Oh my!” Sandy gasped.

“Yeah! And he wanted Tang gone too, but Baba thought of the plan for Tang to stay!” She beamed with obvious pride. “That’s why Tang had to do that rushed confession.”

“I see.” Sandy rubbed his beard. “So did Tang lie as well?”

“No!” Bai He insisted. “I think Tang likes Pigsy. I just know he wouldn’t act like that, but he didn’t lie! Azure lied!” She said with anger. Sandy only nodded his head in understanding so Bai He continued. “So Baba had to leave, and then MK and I overheard Tang tell Pigsy about how Azure was blackmailing Baba! So now we have our plan on getting stuff on Azure so we can blackmail him and get him out!”

“So all four of you thought of this plan last night?”

“No, it was Baba’s plan. He’s looking for stuff himself. He doesn’t know that we know about any of this.” Bai He said feeling anger and sadness well up inside of her.

“Did you want to tell him?”

Bai He looked away from Sandy and back towards the countertop. She swallowed a lump in her throat as she finally responded. “Kind of.”

Letting them sit in silence for a few seconds Sandy then said “You mentioned that Azure lied. Do you think he does not have blackmail on Macaque?”

“No he…he lied to me.” She admitted bitterly, her face twisting up in anger and frustration at herself. “I was such an idiot! He said he was my friend and I believed him!” She dropped her face into her hands, her fingers clenching in sadness and frustration. “I’m so stupid! And I said such mean things to Baba!” She then slammed her fists onto the countertop. “That’s why we need to stay focused. We have to find whatever we can and use it to bring Baba home!”

“Bai He.” Sandy gently called her name as he now leaned his elbows on the other side of the island. “You are not stupid and this is not your fault.”

The young girls face fell as tears immediately sprang to her eyes. “Yes it is!” She cried. “I didn’t talk to him! I didn’t try! I talked to Azure!” She threw out the name like a curse. “He said he was my friend! I was on his side! I wasn’t on my Baba’s side, and now he’s gone!” She cried harder and when Sandy came around she welcomed the hug he offered.

“MK was smart! He stayed away from Azure but not me! I didn’t…I was on…I was wrong! I was dumb!” Sandy shushed the girl and gently combed her hair in a soothing gesture.

“You are not dumb.” He assured firmly. “Unfortunately you met a bad man and he hurt you, MK, and Wukong.”

“That’s why I have to focus! I have to get back at him!” Bai He gripped Sandy’s clothes from where she was hugging the blue giant.

“I understand your motivation but may I offer a suggestion?” Sandy pulled away and then squatted on the floor so he would be at eye level with Bai He. “You regret not talking to Macaque correct?” Bai He gave an ashamed nod of agreement and Sandy only gave her a gentle pat in reassurance. “Perhaps it's time you talk with your Baba instead of keeping everything to yourself.” The young girl shrank inwards but Sandy gave the same gentle and patient tone. “I know it’s hard, I understand you have regrets. I’m sure he does too. But you want to talk with him right?” The young raven’s bottom lip quivered as she gave a very small nod. “You should call your Baba.”

“He’ll be mad at me.” Bai He whispered. “I didn’t try, and when he left-”

“Are you mad at him?” Sandy countered, catching the young girl off guard.

“No!...Well…” She hesitated before answering firmly “No.”

“I think a call between you two is just what you need today.” Sandy smiled.

“What if he asks about how we know? Tang wasn’t supposed to tell us!” She asked, her tone frantic.

“Say whatever you want Bai He, he’s your Baba and he loves you.” The blue giant reassured as he moved to stand up.

Bai He then thought back to her Baba’s departure; how he apologized and then said to never doubt he loved her and MK. She bit her lip then stood up as well.

“I’m going to call him!” She declared and upon seeing the blue giants encouraging smile, her self doubt about talking with her Baba left her mind.

Sandy watched her go to another room so she could call and speak to Macaque in peace. He then poked some of the food as he waited for MK to arrive back in the kitchen. A bit of time later, MK walked back in, his hair still wet.

“Where is everyone? Oh no! Are they strategizing?! Did I miss it?!” MK immediately panicked, he looked all around as if he would be able to spot the team meeting he imagined the 3 others to be at.

“Tang and Pigsy are having, what I hope is, an enlightening conversation. While Bai He is hopefully healing a bit from what happened yesterday.” Sandy said as he gestured for MK to sit down.

“What do you mean?” MK tilted his head as he thought back to Bai He’s words to him just a bit earlier.

“She’s trying a new process instead of pushing her emotions down and chugging forward.” Sandy said as he leaned on the counter near MK.

“Oh…” MK tapped his fingers on the counter and reached for some food before hesitating and pulling back. “But she was right, we don’t have time for that. Baba is alone and we’re just sitting here eating!”

“You know the kittens I sometimes get? They rush and zoom around everywhere.” Sandy smiled and MK nodded and gave a smile of his own, though he was confused by this change in conversation.

“Yeah I know, Mei and I always come whenever you get some turned over to you. They're always so cute!”

“All cats are cute MK!” Sandy gasped in offense.

“But kittens! Come on!” MK pointed exaggeratedly “You know more people come in when you have kittens!”

“That’s true.” The blue giant begrudgingly admitted. “But cats are just as cute! They’re just more grown up.” He waved his hand. “Regardless, Kittens zoom around, expend all their energy in bursts, and mostly never quite get to where they want to go or get what they want.” Sandy looked more meaningfully at MK. “It’s when they learn patience and to look before they leap do they accomplish their goals.”

“.....okay…?” MK’s face fell into one of pure confusion, the metaphor completely going over his head and Sandy’s face fell at that realization.

“What I’m trying to say is, when you rush in, you may be spending your energy on something that you won’t reach from where you currently are.”

“...What does that mean?” MK asked, leaning closer to the blue giant.

“Bai He is talking to Macaque right now because she needs to address with him what happened.” MK’s face fell into shock but Sandy put a hand on his shoulder. “And you also need to deal with what happened. I understand the motivation was not Macaque’s own. But that does not negate what happened and how it affected you, your sister, or anyone.”

MK bit his lip, “But we don’t have time to waste on-”

“On yourself?” Sandy finished for him and the teenager nodded. “It’s not wasting time. You need this.” Seeing MK’s less than convinced face Sandy added. “Have you thought of how this will affect your Dad? Have you discussed this with him?”

“...no.” MK admitted in a guilty tone.

“Because you two haven’t thought everything through.” Sandy said gently. “Too much has happened and you need to process it, not blindly breeze through it.”

“But Baba…” MK trailed off

“You can talk with him as well or ask what Bai He and him discussed once she’s done.” Sandy then sat in the seat next to MK that was previously occupied by Bai He. “For now, why don’t you and I discuss yesterday?”

-

“Wukong we don’t have an app-” Tripitaka began only to stop upon fully opening his front door and getting a good look at the super star. “Are you alright?” He asked, concern now woven into his tone. He extended a hand and gently grabbed Wukong’s upper arm in a grounding and reassuring grip.

“...no.” Wukong muttered under his breath as his shoulders and body slumped, finally unwinding a bit at the admission.

Tripitaka brought Wukong into his home and set the monkey demon up on the armchair. He offered food and drinks but the monkey demon turned him down.

“If you would like to talk about it, I am here.” The bald man offered as he sat on his couch, perpendicular to the chair Wukong was on. With a breath to steel himself Wukong began to explain all that happened.

“You know the worst part?!” Wukong ranted as he continued to pace in front of the couch. Part way through his explanation of yesterday's events, Wukong had shot up and began pacing around the room and gesturing wildly as he retold all that occurred.

Tripitaka only inclined his head as a signal for Wukong to continue and the golden monkey turned to face his manager.

“The worst part is he tried using my words against me!” Wukong stressed as he pointed to himself. “But what I said was we were not right for each other!” Wukong’s eyes did not stray from Tripitaka’s as if this was the most important part that he had to get across. “What he said was we are not right for each other!” Wukong held up both hands and thrust them up and down as if to accentuate his point. “Can you believe it?!” Tripitaka only slowly raised one eyebrow at this apparently critical point. “I mean where did that even come from?!” He began pacing again and threw his arms up into the air. “We were doing great! And again to say I was right!” He blew out a raspberry. “I know I was right! We were bad in the past! I mean you remember!” He pointed at Tripitaka who could only blink before Wukong moved on. “I was right! We were, again” He turned and held eye contact with Tripitaka again “were! Bad for each other!” He began pacing again, “I didn’t say anything about now!” He stressed.

Tripitaka opened his mouth to put in his input but was railroaded by the golden monkey.

“I mean we were doing great! Fantastic even! We teamed up against Pigsy, we had fun with the kids! Hell, we had fun with each other! We hadn’t had any serious arguments!” Wukong threw his arms up and turned to Tripitaka. “And he says we are not good for each other?!” Wukong dragged his hands down his face then glared up at the ceiling. “Saying I was right as if I had already said we…As if I was the one-!” Wukong grumbled and glared but apparently couldn’t find it in himself to finish the sentence. Finally he screamed out, his tone pure anger. “He’s the one that left again!” The golden monkey was breathing harshly and turned to look at his friend.

Tripitaka blinked and in that millisecond Wukong’s entire demeanor and expression broke. In place of anger there was now defeat. His shoulders hanging low, his arms useless at his sides, as if he didn’t have the energy to lift them. “He left.” He breathed out, his gaze seeming to go hazy, as if becoming lost in a fog. In another breath, he added quieter and in a much more defeated tone. “...He left me again.”

Tripitaka took a slow breath and gave Wukong a sympathetic look as he patted the spot next to him on the couch. Wukong slowly trudged over and plopped himself down, ungracefully. Like a puppet that had no strings or will to hold themselves up anymore.

Wukong looked to be lost in his thoughts and the bald man let them sit in silence for a few minutes. When he noticed Wukong coming back to himself, he gently grabbed Wukong’s hand and gave it a squeeze. Once he confirmed he had Wukong’s full attention he spoke.

“I will take care of Bai He’s school and minimize the press coverage on MK.”

Wukong smiled at his friend in relief and thankfulness. His friend and manager was not the best socially but he tried and Wukong appreciated him for…well everything.

“However, before I begin any of that.” Tripitaka’s face became serious yet he still kept a hold of Wukong’s hand. “I require you to go through the storage locker of Macaque’s belongings.”

“...What? Why?” Wukong tilted his head in confusion at the odd request.

“You have to go through it all.” The bald man continued, not addressing the monkey demon’s questions. Wukong’s face was still a wash of confusion as the manager patted Wukong’s hand before letting it go.

“Seriously?” Wukong questioned as he watched Tripitaka stand up and gather the two pieces of paper Wukong had brought over.

“It does not have to be today nor tomorrow. You can even bring the children if you wish.” His friend smiled at him. “However I am indeed serious.”

Wukong scratched the back of his head in confusion. “What does that have to do with anything?”

“Would you like some tea? I am certain you need it after all that talking.” The bald man said kindly then walked into his kitchen, leaving the monkey demon behind. The golden monkey grumbled for a bit but did yell out what tea he would like.

Chapter End Notes

Chapter 15: Call in the Squad

Chapter Notes

Day 2

See the end of the chapter for more notes

“Hey MK! You feel like going riding today?” Wukong asked with a smile as he bounced over toward his son. However upon seeing MK’s grimace the golden monkey figured he was going to get a rejection.

“Sorry Dad.” MK said “I actually have plans with Mei.”

“That’s ok.” Wukong rallied with an encouraging smile. “Man, first Bai He and then you, I’m striking out!” He laughed and MK chuckled with him.

Wukong remained standing in the teenagers' doorway as he watched his son get ready for the day. Hesitantly and almost nervously he spoke again, trying to keep his body posture relaxed.

“I know you and Bai He were talking with Sandy most of yesterday evening but, if you want, you know you can talk to me.” He gave a smile he hoped looked open and inviting as he leaned stiffly against the doorframe.

With his back to his dad, MK tried to hide his grimace of guilt. He felt bad, he really did about not talking with his Dad at all about any of the revelations or the plan he and Bai He were working on.

Yesterday he had barely seen him, his day was spent mostly with Sandy and his sister. Though they did all re-group up around dinner time when Wukong, Sandy, MK, and Bai He bullied Pigsy and Tang out the door; forcing the two to go out on a date. It had been a moment filled with laughs and jibes at the two butlers' expense. Wukong had walked the two all the way to the car to ensure they had left the property, since Tang and Pigsy were both putting up a fuss over going out and leaving the kids. While he was out MK had pulled Bai He and Sandy aside so they could talk. Time seemed to have passed in a blur since once they were done talking, it was quite late. Wukong had left 3 plates, a large bowl of noodles, and a pot of pasta sauce on the kitchen counter with a note saying he made the pasta and wanted them all to at least have one plate of pasta before bed. MK had peaked in on him in his office last night; but his Dad was in his work mode; where his focus was zeroed in on whatever project was in front of him.

And now here was his Dad trying to reach out to MK and he’d have to brush him off. Biting his tongue to try and distract him from the guilt he turned to face the golden monkey.

“I know Dad. It’s just…” He waved his hands about and Wukong immediately began nodding.

“No I-I get it!” He held up his hands in surrender. “I don’t mean to put pressure on you.”

“No you’re not!”

“MK it’s fine, don’t worry, don’t worry.” He chanted as he came up and pulled the fretting teen into a fast hug. “I just want you guys to know I’m here whenever you’re ready.”

Wukong made to pull away and couldn’t stop the startled noise from escaping him as MK clung to him, yanking him back full force into a proper hug.

“I love you Dad.” MK muttered into the golden monkey's shoulder.

“Aw Bud.” Wukong smiled and gripped the teenager tighter. “I love you too.”

-

Telling everything to Mei was both the best thing and the worst. It felt terrible recounting that night everything they had worked on this summer seemingly fell apart. And he was helpless to stop Mei’s rage even while trying to defend his Baba. Then once he told her about the mystery blackmail, Mei's rage at Macaque had simmered and turned full force to Azure. However, Mei was a gossip and so of course her main focus became pondering what the blackmail could be. MK tried to squash that but knew it was a futile mission. So he tried to shift her focus on the mission he and Bai He had come up with.

They were still going to follow through with their blackmail on Azure plan. Yet they wouldn’t just boldly go up to him with it. They would then loop Macaque and Wukong in. Then together they would all decide what to do next.

“We should call Red Son!” Mei beamed.

“I tried! He didn’t answer.” MK pouted. “And this isn’t something to leave on the answering machine.”

“Boooooo Red Son!” Mei then bounced up and dashed out of the room. MK quirked an eyebrow but only waited for his friend to return. When she did come back she smiled a shit eating grin but didn’t elaborate on where she had run off to.

“So where is Bai He?” She asked taking her seat next to MK again.

“She’s doing recon!” MK said dramatically.

“What does that mean?”

“She’s with Azure.”At Mei’s full body jolt and bug eyes reaction MK quickly held up his hands to calm his friend. “It was her idea, she wanted to. I tried to talk to her but there was no changing her mind.”

MK then directed Mei back to the issue at hand, namely trying to think of how to find information on Azure. They had debated ideas for about an hour when one of the people working at Mei’s mansion came up to her with a phone.

“Oh finally!” Mei cheered and then hit the speaker button.

“MEI!” Red Son’s furious voice screamed out after Mei had given a cheeky greeting. “You called my house every three minutes for an hour?!”

“No! I had Jules do it. MK and I have been strategizing.”

“You can’t just hog up the phone lines like that Mei!” Red Son continued to scream. “My Father was expecting a message and may have missed it with your constant calling!”

“Awww,” Mei cooed. “Tell DBK Daddy I said sorry.” MK snickered into his hand as he tried to hide his laughter.

“Do NOT call my Father that!” Red Son screeched making MK and Mei burst into laughter. “I’m hanging up.” Red Son said in a deadpan tone.

“Wait, wait, wait!” MK rushed. “I need your help! Please don’t hang up!”

After a deep sigh Red Son responded. “Let me call you on the other line.”

“Do it quickly or we’ll just call you back.” Mei sing-songed, laughing at the muttered ‘Bitch’ that Red Son gave before hanging up.

Upon calling back, MK and Mei had filled him in on everything that happened.

“It’s truly astounding how these monkey demons have so much drama surrounding them. My family does not have this much drama and neither side can stand the other.” Red Son said after being briefed on everything that had occurred and the current plan.

“Hey!’ MK defended albeit weakly.

“So got any more bright ideas on how we can dig up some dirt?” Mei asked.

“I would not qualify following him around and blatantly asking currently unidentified people about any possible information they may have on Azure, as ideas.” Red Son said with derision. “Let alone bright ideas.”

Mei glared at the phone while MK thunked his head on the table. “I don’t know what else to do.” He whined.

“Yeah, excuse us for not knowing how to be conniving.” Mei said while still glaring at the phone, and hoping Red Son could sense it.

“You pay people off.” Red Son said in a matter of fact tone. “And you do not want it immediately linked back to you, you need a plausible fall guy.” MK gave Mei a concerned look, while Mei just rolled her eyes.

“Oh teach us, great evil one.” Mei mocked, making MK chuckle.

“You’re the ones who asked for my help!” Red Son yelled.

“I did! And I do need it!” MK interrupted as he covered Mei’s mouth to prevent his friend from antagonizing Red Son further. “So what should I do?”

“I” Red Son stressed the pronoun pointedly. “Will make some calls. Then I will let you know what I find when I arrive.”

“Arrive?!” Mei and MK questioned.

“Of course I’m heading over you dimwits!” MK and Mei could picture the red head screaming at the phone as if it had personally offended him. “Someone has to hold you two back from your own imbecilic tendencies.” He scoffed. “I bet your next plan was to call Azure up and just spend time with him hoping to glean some vital information.”

Silence followed Red Son’s words and after a pregnant pause Red Son spoke up again. “...You can’t be serious.”

“Hey! That wasn’t my idea! It was Bai He’s!” MK ratted his sister out and ignored Mei shaking her head next to him.

“The nine year old?!”

“She’s eleven!” MK shot back.

“That’s not helping your case, noodle brain!” Red Son fired back. “Bai He that is an unwise choice, Azure is clearly very good at navigating-”

“She’s not here my dude.” Mei interrupted.

“Where is she?!”

Mei looked over to MK for him to answer, her face one of judgment. MK scratched behind his head and leaned his head back while letting out a long sigh. Hearing said sigh, Red Son spoke up.

“You can’t be- are you serious?!” Then Red Son’s voice now sounded farther away as if he held the phone away from himself. “Mother, I require the private jet as soon as possible.”

“Noodle brain, listen to me very carefully!” Red Son now spoke back into the phone properly. “The only research you should do is with your trusted people. People who may know something but would not turn on you for any money or anything in the world.”

“Tripitaka.” MK immediately answered.

“Tripitaka?” Mei questioned.

“He knows everything about their industry and he’s basically my Uncle.” MK explained. “I could ask what he knows about Azure.”

“Do not be blunt about it. Ask casual questions and try to discern the nuances of his answers.” Red Son explained between the sounds of clothes being packed.

“Discern the…what?” MK tilted his head at the phone and Mei grabbed his head in a headlock.

“Don’t worry man, I’ll help you out!” She cheered.

“Also do not immediately-”

“Will do, thanks for the advice Red Boy!” Mei interrupted.

“I said do not and I didn’t even finish-” Red Son ranted only to be cut off as Mei hung up the phone with a shit eating grin. MK laughed and as both friends imagined Red Son’s reaction to being hung up on they laughed harder.

-

“I’m so sorry again for having to do this Bai He.” Azure apologized again as he walked the girl inside Yellow Tusks home. “I promise this will be quick and then we can resume our day.” He beamed and the young girl only gave a polite nod and smile back.

“Ah thank you for coming brother.” Yellow Tusk greeted. “Oh and who is this?”

“This is Bai He, she’s a friend of mine.” Azure introduced. “Bai He, this is one of my brothers Yellow Tusk.”

Bai He took the offered hand politely albeit with some confusion. “Brother?”

Azure and Yellow Tusk laughed at the confusion while walking inside. Inside sat Peng and a box for a bookshelf. Azure and Bai He had been spending the day together when he remembered he had promised to help his brothers build some new furniture. He had promised the young girl it wouldn’t take long and that he would not reveal her parentage as it was still a secret. Bai He had accepted his promise and agreed to go with him; and so now she found herself in Azure’s brother’s home.

“This is my other sibling Peng, Peng this is my friend Bai He.” Peng only nodded at the young girl who nodded back. “All three of us grew up together, so much so we stopped being friends and became family.”

“Oh,” The young girl nodded in understanding. “So you guys kind of adopted each other?”

“Exactly!” Azure grinned. He then bent down to be more at her level and gave a wink “Just another thing we have in common.”

Bai He’s head twitched to the side but she smiled through it and gave a small nod in agreement. Tusk then came up to the young girl and asked if he could get her a drink or a snack.

At first while building the bookshelf Bai He kept silent, even as Azure and his siblings tried to cajole her into a conversation she kept to herself and was reserved.

“You don’t talk much do you?” Peng commented after getting a one word answer to their previous question.

“Peng!” Azure reprimanded as Bai He cast her eyes down in a look of shame. “Bai He’s just shy by nature.”

“Why don’t you help me out in the kitchen?” Yellow Tusk offered in a kind tone. “It’s past lunch time so let’s make some sandwiches or something.” He held out an arm to guide the young girl as he asked after what food she would like to make and eat.

Once she and Yellow Tusk were in the kitchen Peng silently mouthed to Azure. “We are not getting anything from her. She’s too tight-lipped.”

“Give it time.” Azure silently mouthed back with confidence to which Peng only rolled their eyes.

In the kitchen, as Bai He was cutting slices of cheese she bit her lip then glanced up at Yellow Tusk. The elephant smiled and continued to sprinkle spices and various ingredients into a stew he was making. Finally Bai He took a deep breath and approached the man.

“Azure says we’re a lot alike.” Yellow Tusk made a sound of understanding so Bai He elaborated. “B-but I’m not sure. He’s so confident and has you all and isn’t shy like me.” She looked down at the floor and Yellow Tusk bit his own lip.

“W-well you are alike in other ways, I’m sure.” He answered stiffly and so Bai He let out a disappointed sigh.

Then an idea clicked in her head and she asked “Was Azure like me when he was younger?”

“Shy?”

“No, well yes but not just that…” Bai He fiddled with her hair as she thought. “Just…what was he like when he was younger?”

“Well he wasn’t shy I can tell you that.” Yellow Tusk responded as he turned back to his soup. “That’s not to say being shy is bad or anything but Azure has always had a way with people, bringing them in and making friends.”

“So he has a lot of friends?”

“We used to.”

“What happened?”

“Ah the usual adult things. We grew apart, interests changed, and it didn’t help with us moving so far.” Yellow Tusk answered as he stirred in some salt.

“Oh you guys moved?” The young girl perked up visibly “Where from?”

“Originally we are from Oklahoma.”

“I’m from New York!” Bai He pipped in. “So I guess we also have moving around as something in common.” Yellow Tusk nodded his head in agreement so Bai He came closer and leaned against the counter near the larger man. “Was it hard for him and you guys when you first came here?”

“We had our challenges but together we made it through.” Yellow Tusk smiled and Bai He gave him a strained smile back.

Chapter 16: Tick Tick ...

Chapter Notes

Day 3

See the end of the chapter for more notes

“Hey kids!” Wukong greeted as he came into the kitchen with a pep in his step. “If you have plans today, cancel them! Because your Dad is taking you both to our beach!” He boasted with a huge grin. Yet his smile quickly fell at MK’s guilty looking face. “Again?”

“Sorry Dad.” MK did look guilty. “Red Son is in town so him, Mei and I are hanging out.”

“Well more fun for Bai He and me then!” Wukong teased but the young girl looked less than excited. “What’s wrong?” He turned to the young girl who was biting her lip.

“Could we go to the library instead?” Bai He asked hopefully.

“The library?” Wukong questioned, his tone screaming he did not like that idea.

Bai He averted her eyes and stared down at the countertop. “Baba and I would go to the library a lot and I haven’t been able to go to one since before camp.”

“Oh.” Wukong scratched the back of his head. “Well, yeah, yeah! Of course we can!” He grinned and then went and wrapped his arm around his daughter in a half hug. “We’ll wear amazing disguises.” He boasted and the young girl giggled at his exaggeration. “And peruse all the literature!” He finished in a mock fancy tone.

It got Bai He to laugh more and Wukong counted that as a victory.

“Eat your breakfast you drama king.” Pigsy grouched from the stove as he worked on the omelette he was making.

When Tang joined the group for breakfast, he quickly asked if he could tag along with the two; to which they both agreed.

Wukong and Bai He entered the library in their disguises, Wukong muttering a secret agent tune as they walked. Bei He giggled at it while Tang shook his head at the antics. He had worn one of his regular outfits and declined the offer for him to wear a hat or to trade his glasses in for sunglasses.

“So what area do we want to check out?” Wukong asked as they walked around the library lobby.

It was vast and open and showed the multiple staircases and glass elevator that would take you to the 4 levels that comprised the library. On the far side of the lobby was a large desk with numerous librarians working. Around them people walked around, not paying the trio anymind, other than being sure to not walk into them.

“Ummm.” Bai He bit her lip as she looked around the huge structure. “How about….well…” She was halted in her steps as she realized she wasn’t sure where to look. She had wanted to go to the library to look into finding possible information on Azure but now that she was here, she didn’t know where to actually look or how to start.

“How about we start with the history section?” Tang offered, keen to read or even re-read some of his favorite biographies and autobiographies that he had read in the past.

“No.” Bai He shook her head “Not history, maybe…uh…maybe…” She fidgeted with her hair as she thought, till Wukong put a gentle hand on her shoulder.

“How about we start at the teens section?” He offered with a smile. “If you can’t find anything there then we can move on.”

“Ok.” She smiled and with minimal complaints from Tang they made their way over to the teen section which was on the second floor.

When there, Wukong had watched Bai He for a time before giving her space and perusing the bookshelves himself to see if any cover art piqued his interest. Tang looked around in boredom but the teens section had a plethora of magazines and with a force of habit Tang grabbed one and scanned through it, looking for photos of MK.

Bai He sidled over to Tang and quietly tried to garner his attention without alerting Wukong. With a firm tug on his shirt Tang looked over to the young girl with a smile but that quickly fell when he noticed her distressed face.

“Bai He? Is something wrong?”

“I don’t know what to do.” She whispered urgently. “I want to try and find more information on Azure and maybe his life in Oklahoma but I don’t know how! I figured I could look in the library.” She gestured around, stress obvious on her young face. “But I don’t know what I’m looking for! I don’t even know how to find what to look for! Or even where to look!”

“Woah, woah!” Tang quickly held up his arms in surrender. “Calm down, it’s ok. It’s alright!”

As Tang tried to make sure Bai He took some calming breaths. Wukong had noticed the two talking and Bai He looking distressed. He bit his lip and wanted to make his way over but instead looked back at the series of books he had been looking at previously. He didn’t want to pressure his daughter. She was having issues opening up again and this whole thing with Macaque clearly affected her. Plus if she used to go to the library with Macaque she may be reminiscing with Tang and Wukong did not want to disturb that. Wukong glanced back and upon seeing Tang and Bai He talking again he nodded to himself, secure in the knowledge the young girl looked calmer and less upset than previously. Narrowing his eyes in determination he glanced around the teen section before taking off.

Meanwhile Tang had calmed the young raven from getting too worked up and in her own head. And with gentle hands on her shoulders Tang told Bai He he would take over the research.

“You may not know how to research deep dives like this yet, but I do.” He smiled and tilted his glasses. “Leave the library research to me.”

“But then what do I do?”

Tang glanced back to where he had last seen Wukong and then gave Bai He a grin. “Go mess with your Dad.”

“Just-?”

“Yes, just go have fun with him.” He gave her a gentle push. “Even If you guys want to leave and go shopping or whatever just come back for me before heading home.”

Bai He went to say something but Wukong fast walking over to them with a few books in his arms stopped her. “Dad? What’s that?”

“These!” He lifted some comic books in his arms up. “Are some great comics!” He then grabbed another book from the pile in his arms “And this is apparently a good romance book, if you want to read that.” He then grabbed another book. “Or I grabbed this, apparently this is a good mystery book. I asked a librarian if they knew of any popular books and they gave me these options. I wasn’t sure which genre you liked so I grabbed all of them!” He beamed.

Bai He grabbed the mystery book. “I love stories about mysteries, and I haven’t read this one yet!”

“Great! And now I know where that section is so we can head that way!” The disguised monkey demon smiled and gestured for Bai He to follow him.

“I’ll be heading to do my own reading.” Tang called to the two before they could get to far. “I already told Bai He but even if you guys want to leave I’ll be in here so just come pick me up before you head home.”

With that Father and daughter went off, and Tang turned with a glint in his glasses as he began to plot out where to begin.

-

“Red Son!” MK screamed as he ran at his friend. The redhead barely had enough time to drop the briefcase he had been holding and brace himself before MK had jumped on the taller teenager.

“M-MK!” Red screeched as he righted himself. “G-get off me!” He tried to push his clingy friend away but then let out an “oompf” as he was tackled on his other side by a jubilant Mei.

“You finally showed up!” Mei greeted from her place on his back/left side.

“Would you-” Red Son tried to say while struggling to detach his two friends from his person.

“Mei is he holding you up too?!” MK asked as he tried to angle himself to check if Mei’s feet were on the floor or not.

“Don’t move-” Red Son tried but was steamrolled over.

“Yeah, who knew little Red Boy had the ability.” Mei teased while wearing a shit eating grin.

“EXCUSE ME?!” The redhead screamed in a tone of pure offense. “I train with my Father! You think I wouldn’t be able to handle holding two meager twigs such as yourselves?!”

“Hot damn.” MK whispered as he glanced from how Red Son did indeed seem to not struggle at all, even while having both his and Mei’s weight fully on him.

However the whispered words were lost as Mei had squished her face closer to Red Son and spoke louder. “Yes yes, we’re all very impressed, your weight lifting highness.”

“Get off me!” Red Son then began to once again try and pry the two friends off himself. This time MK and Mei did eventually acquiesce.

Once the three friends calmed down they moved into one of the many sitting room’s Mei’s family had. There they began getting to the point of the matter.

“I assume you gathered nothing.” Red Son said derisively.

“Actually we learned a lot!” Mei countered. Yet MK scratched his head with a look of doubt.

“Well not alot but we got some information.” With a gesture to elaborate, MK began naming off what they learned yesterday. “Bai He found out that Azure has two brothers Peng and Yellow Tusk. And that they moved here from-”

“Oklahoma.” Red Son finished looking unimpressed. “Is that it?”

“No!” MK fired back, defensive.

“Bai He broke Azure’s car!” Mei added with a proud smile.

“She what?!” Red Son’s attention swiveled over towards Mei, who rocked backwards in glee at the memory.

“Yeah,” MK scratched the side of his face. He was not best pleased with Bai He’s actions but she was unrepentant when she told him of what she had done. “Apparently she poured water in his gas tank. Evidently a lot” He held his hands up to indicate a large amount. “His car broke down and Dad had to drive out to go pick her up yesterday.” Red Son nodded his head in approval as did Mei, and MK briefly questioned his friend's morals.

“I hope she was stealthy about that.” Red Son said in a sharp tone full of warning.

“This is Bai He!” Mei waved the concern away. “She’s super sneaky! Remember camp?!”

“True.” Red Son drawled.

MK then coughed and in an exaggerated gesture of getting his friend's attention back on himself he began again. “Back to Azure; Tripitaka said Azure always works well with his fellow actors, but evidently support staff hate working with him.”

“Now that’s interesting.” Red Son leaned forward with interest. “Did he mention any names of who may particularly hate the man? Or anyone working with him now?”

MK shook his head in the negative. “No names, he said it’s just what he heard from his network. Oh!” MK jolted to sit up. “And apparently he’s been through a few Managers. He evidently always thinks they aren’t trying enough for him or something.”

“The old managers could be another lead.” Mei smiled, but her smile fell when Red Son scoffed.

“You two are so lucky you have me.” He drawled with a menacing glint to his glasses.

“Oh here we go.” Mei muttered under her breath.

Red Son then reached for the briefcase he had and opened it with a flourish.

“Here are transcripts of interviews I had obtained from Azure’s past two Managers Manjusri and Wuji.” He held up each folder then after an appropriate amount of “ohs” and “ahs” from MK (Mei was rolling her eyes) he dug further into his briefcase. “And here is what I could find about their life here.” He placed that folder in front of the others as well, then brought a hand to his face. “I wasn’t able to look into anything from Oklahoma though. My family and I do not have contacts there. Plus I only had a day.”

“This is great!” MK cheered. “I can’t believe you found all this!”

Red Son was soaking up the praise and Mei rolled her eyes and whole upper body. “Don’t compliment him too much MK, we can’t have his head inflate anymore.”

“You listen here Pony-girl!”

“I haven’t had a pony in years!”

-

After a long day of reading, taking notes, making calls, and reading some more Tang felt a familiar ache in the back of his neck. Yet he couldn't say he hated it. He enjoyed deep diving into research, it was still his passion if not his job anymore. Wukong, Bai He, and himself had come back late and ate dinner with Pigsy. MK had called and said how he would spend the night at Mei’s that night.

Now Tang was sprawled on top of his bed, staring at the ceiling in dismay. This trip had started so nice, and he and Pigsy had a genuine connection growing throughout his time here. Or well, he thought they did. The man let out an aggravated breath before grabbing a pillow and muffling a loud groan into it.

The day after Macaque had left, the family had pushed him and Pigsy out to go on a date. Tang and been hopeful this would start their connection and romance back up. Though those hopes were dashed when Pigsy had turned to him and point blank said to not think of this as a date. That this was just to keep the ruse going. He had tried, once again, to explain his feelings for Pigsy were never a ruse yet the stubborn pig demon didn’t even acknowledge his words.

It hurt. It hurt more than any prior rejection he had received and it was because he was really really taken with Pigsy. His gruff demeanor that hid a soft heart, a brilliant cook, and a man that stood firm in his principles. If nothing else this frustration fueled his anger at Azure. If Azure hadn’t done what he did, then he and Pigsy could let things play out as they were and then maybe….but who knows. Maybe he would still have been rejected. He groaned again and was tempted to wallow in self pity longer.

However he also wanted to call Macaque about the information he found or began to find. He would need to research further.

However upon Macaque answering, Tang immediately knew as frustrated and upset as he felt, it could not compare to how Macaque was feeling. He could tell just by the monkey’s tone of voice, that he was very low.

“Yeah we spent the whole day there.” Tang had just finished giving a summary of how the day went, keeping the ebony monkey in the loop on his childrens lives.

“I can’t believe Wukong stayed all day in a library.” Macaque’s tone wreaked of disbelief. “I can’t even do that, it’s so stiff!”

“It is not!” Tang immediately defended. “Libraries are fun!”

“Well hope you didn’t fall in love with that library. If what you told me about Wukong making all those crafts in the toddler section really did happen you all are probably blacklisted.” The ebony monkey laughed.

“No I think they enjoyed them. The one worker even helped hang the paper chain he had made.” Tang refuted and Macaque only gave a hum back before the two fell into silence.

Bracing himself Tang began to try and explain what he had begun to find about Azure. However Macaque interrupted him.

“You told them, didn’t you?” Macaque questioned, and it jarred Tang with how out of left field the question was.

“What? Told who what?”

There was a deep intake of breath on the other end, and in an even tone the ebony monkey said “You told the kids about the blackmail, didn’t you?!”

“Whaaaaatttt? No! Absolutely not!” Silence greeted Tang and so he kept lamenting how he did not and would never.

Only to be cut off when Macaque finally responded. “Bai He called me the other day, and the way she ended the call kept nagging at the back of my brain.” He paused and Tang squeezed his eyes shut in a wince. “She said to not worry, and that she and MK remembered to never let anyone get away with crossing them.” Tang’s mouth screwed up and they sat in silence for probably a whole minute.

“You told them, didn’t you?

“Ok so maybe they eavesdropped when I told PIgsy and-

“You told Pigsy?!” Macaque’s tone up to this point had been tempered, not calm but not angry. However this statement, he screeched in anger.

“Not everything!” Tang rushed to defend himself. “Just that Azure blackmailed you into leaving!”

“You had two jobs Tang! Don’t tell and watch the kids! I asked for those two things!” Macaque screamed so loud Tang held the phone away from his face.

“I didn’t give the details! They don’t know what Azure has, just that he made you leave!” A distant scream and curse could be heard as Tang continued to explain. “I wasn’t planning on telling them! They snuck down and eavesdropped on me and Pigsy.”

“Why were you telling Pigsy-”

“Because he didn’t believe me! He still doesn’t believe me!” Now Tang was yelling, though he quickly caught himself and got up to open his bedroom door and ensure no one was on the other side or in the hallway listening. When he picked the landline back up, Macaque was silent. Clearly waiting for Tang to come back to the call.

“He doesn’t believe you?” Macaque questioned slowly. “He thinks I would just leave for no reason?”

“No, no!” Tang sighed and rubbed the back of his neck. “He believes that. He doesn’t believe me! He doesn’t think I have genuine feelings for him.” He groaned and flopped back onto the bed. “...I think he actually hates me now.”

“What?” Macaque sounded so baffled that it caused Tang to chuckle.

“Wukong and the kids pushed us to go on a date the other day and the way he acted the whole time. I thought there were moments that maybe meant he heard me and we could….I don’t know, but…he acted like it was such a chore.” Tang’s lip quivered.

“Tang.”

“I tried talking to him,” Tang rushed out, his tone wobbly, “but I don’t know if he’s just not listening to me or just thinks I’m constantly lying now.”

“He’s an idiot.” Macaque deadpanned and Tang barked a laugh. “Him and Wukong are alike in that. They’re so damn stubborn that once they think they are right, you can’t change them.”

“Well he thinks I’m a liar…and apparently I can’t change that.”

“You can.” Macaque assured. Tang let out a breath that must have sounded like defeat to the ebony monkey since Macaque then growled. “I’m going to kill Azure.”

“Ah, speaking of him.” Tang sat up and went to grab his notes. “I found some information we need to look into.”

Day 4

See the end of the chapter for more notes

“Hey Al!” Wukong called out to the lion demon that looked like he was on a mission. However upon the monkey demon's greeting he startled and blinked a few times before focusing on Wukong approaching. “Listen, I know this summer has been crazy.” Wukong scratched the back of his head and chuckled. “And I was not expecting it at all! But-” Wukong reached out and held Azure’s hand. “The kids are all at Mei’s today. So do you want to have a date? We could-”

Azure cut him off as he firmly grasped Wukong’s hand and beamed at the monkey demon in excitement. “Announce our relationship?! I already have several ideas about who we should interview with first!” Azure seemed to come to life as he began rattling off ideas and plans about their announcement.

“No, no.” Wukong stopped him as gently as he could and squeezed their joined hands as he watched Azure’s excitement dim. “I’m still not ready for that. I’m still working on everything with Bai He and MK.” Azure rolled his eyes and Wukong felt hurt and angry at the act. “Look everything…” The monkey took a breath and let go of his anger. “Everything is just a lot right now,” He sighed, feeling the weight of it all fall back on his shoulders. “but we have some time.” He squeezed their joined hands. “And it’s been awhile so…” He trailed off but upon looking at Azure’s face, his own fell.

Azure looked entirely displeased, not disappointed which Wukong would have understood. But no, he looked displeased and irritated. He brushed off Wukong’s hand and then put on a look of disappointment. Yet, behind the disappointment, Wukong could see the first expression hidden in Azure’s eyes.

“I know you’re going through a lot Wukong, but how do you think I feel?” Azure intoned. “I found out you’ve been hiding a child from me! And that you invited your ex to live with you! And you still don’t want to announce our relationship?! Have you thought about how this has been on me? How much I’ve had to take on and not have you give anything back?!” Azure spread his arms wide and still wore the expression of disappointment.

Wukong, instead of feeling the guilt that he maybe should. He only felt taken aback. Shocked at Azure’s words and…audacity. He completely dismissed what Wukong is and has been going through. And to phrase it as if Wukong pushed this on him when in fact Wukong tried to keep him away. He kept him distant so he himself could get things settled and figured out before bringing Azure in. But no Azure wouldn’t let that happen!

Azure’s face quickly melted into confusion and caution as Wukong glared at him. Perhaps sensing he made a misstep Azure went to say something and to touch Wukong; but the monkey stepped back.

“You were the one who wouldn't listen when I told you I had a family matter to deal with! You were the one who refused to listen to me!”

“Wukong, wait-” Azure looked panicked but Wukong was not having it. He let Azure have his say now he would get his.

“And now you want to say you had a lot to “take on”?!” Wukong held up air quotes as he threw the lion’s words back at him. “You pushed in when I was not ready!” Wukong’s eyes quickly darted around before zeroing back in on Azure. “And you’re doing it again! You’re pushing this announcement when I’ve said several times I’m not ready!”

“I misspoke, I didn’t mean-!” Azure tried to grab Wukong again and this time the monkey let him hold his hand. “Wukong I didn’t mean it like that! I was only…” Azure looked to be thinking a mile a minute “I was just… It was all…” the lingering silence and failed articulation did nothing to quell Wukong’s ire.

“Oh!” A new voice greeted, and both actors turned to find Tripitaka standing there having just walked into the room the two were in. “Apologies I didn’t mean to intrude.” He glanced between Azure and Wukong, who slowly separated from each other.

“It’s fine.” Wukong sighed, taking his hand back and rubbing it with his other hand.

“Well Wukong I need you to come with me.” The bald man said, tone reeking of pure business.

“Does this involve…?” Wukong trailed off and Tripitaka nodded once in response.

Feeling the dread of phone calls and paperwork Wukong groaned and did an exaggerated head roll. He was not looking forward to the school interviews and calls to the press. He glanced over to Azure and expected to see the lion look anxious or upset at them not being able to clear the air. However Azure only looked…different. He couldn’t put his finger on what he would call the look Azure had. Perhaps frustrated but not at Tripitaka so maybe him? Yet that didn’t even seem the right word. He wasn’t sure what it was, but he knew one thing. He was happy to be leaving.

Saying a short goodbye Wukong followed Tripitaka out and into his car.

“So what schools do you have it narrowed down to?” Wukong asked, moving to recline back in the passenger seat. Tripitaka only gave Wukong a look with a raised eyebrow. “What?” the monkey asked.

“Did you forget what I said?” The manager asked but didn’t wait for an answer before continuing “We’re going to the storage units for you to go through all Macaque’s items.”

“What?! You were serious?!”

“You know I always am.”

“I was hoping you were joking.” Wukong grumbled as he slouched in his seat.

-

Azure looked after Wukong’s back and his frustration grew tenfold! He was already pissed off but this whole conversation and complete derailment of his plans only made his mood worse!

During his day out with Bai He, his car broke down. Upon taking it to a mechanic he was told the issue was a sizable amount of water in his gas tank. Now he at first guessed it may have been someone random, or a vengeful employee or associate. Yet, he hadn’t been on any set and his car went into the garage at night; leaving a very small window for anyone to do it. But Yellow Tusk remembered Bai He going outside to get some air with a full glass of water and coming back with it empty.
